**What it is.** A local desktop workspace for mathematical and technical documents. Typst, Markdown (print profile) and LaTeX (pdfLaTeX/LuaLaTeX) sources are edited in Monaco and directly on the compiled pages. Both editors share one revisioned document service and one undo history. Python, R and Julia code runs in Jupyter kernels (a project-local Python environment can be set up from the Research page), and the resulting figures and tables can be inserted into documents. Lean files are checked with the project's own toolchain; the verdict comes from a two-step audit that re-checks the file's declarations with the kernel without running the file's code, and the Lean panel lists exactly which declarations were checked. A Lake package that uses Mathlib is prepared by a guided setup the user starts (pinned toolchain, pinned dependencies, Mathlib's prebuilt cache), and Lean does not start on one that is not prepared. Agent tasks run on the Anthropic Messages API or the OpenAI Responses API (each with the user's API key), with attachments (text, images, PDF), and the application gates every tool call by scope, consent, permission profile, execution mode, approval mode (Ask, or Auto within permissions the user chose for the project) and budgets; changes stay reviewable and revertible, and agent responses are rendered as inert Markdown with math. Every project has a typed project graph, kept reconciled with its files and open buffers, that the Graph, Research, and Agents pages and agent tasks read and change through one validated service. New projects are created from presets or an objective. Workflow drafts are authored in a form or on a canvas and validated in Rust; a saved, well-formed revision runs only from a run plan the user reviews in full and starts (or, unattended, from a standing plan the user approved for a trigger), one step at a time or up to 4 independent steps at once within application-wide limits, with bounded composites, conditions, and repeats; its Python, R, and Julia steps can run in a local Docker container that sees only their declared inputs, every attempt records its environment, and a run can be exported as a manifest or reproduced from its recorded inputs. Files, research files, workflows, run and task records, and graph items can be deleted, copied and pasted, and duplicated from context menus and focus-scoped shortcuts; one backend plans every such operation, asks only when it has a concrete consequence in the project (one combined decision), moves files to the Recycle Bin (never silently deleting permanently), keeps app records restorable in Settings › Storage & History, and restores without overwriting. Native tools run only in folders the user trusts. Agent code runs without a per-call approval only in a local Docker container, or natively when the user granted the project native code for Auto tasks or approved native code for the rest of a task.

**Notation.** Sizes are binary and written with their unit: KiB, MiB, GiB (1 KiB = 1024 bytes). The interface shows a size with one decimal ("48.2 KiB", "3.4 MiB") and bytes below 1 KiB as "812 B" (`src/lib/format.ts`); messages written in Rust say "812 bytes" there. Durations read "850 ms", "1.5 s", "45 s", "1 min 15 s", "2 h 5 min"; dates use the locale's medium style ("Nov 20, 2026", with a time "Nov 20, 2026, 2:03 PM").

**At a glance**
- IPC commands: **193 release + 16 debug-only** (209 handlers; 193 `allow-*` grants)
- Events: **24 channels** (16 with payloads + 8 resync channels); 9 event enums with 50 variants, plus the structs `TrustChanged`, `BuildEvent`, `BuildStatusEvent`, `WorkflowRunEvent`, `WorkflowTriggerEvent`, `FileImportProgress`, and the `project-op` payload `OpResult`
- Command palette: **75 registered commands** (74 listed; Show All Commands hides itself); 25 keybindings
- Agent tools: **16**; 34 gate refusal codes; 60 distinct `agents.` codes; 10 task statuses; 8 tool-call states; 2 approval modes (Ask, Auto); 3 allowance kinds
- Settings keys: **29 persisted** (App 2, Editor 7, Project 4, Execution 9, Provider 5, Trust 3) + one API key per provider (OS store) + 7 frontend-only; plus app-local records: project permissions (Auto), the remembered research environment per project, unfinished-setup records (§9)
- e2e scenarios: **105** files in `scripts/e2e/scenarios/` (helpers live one folder up and are not counted; 94 in the default run; the 11 `live-*` scenarios (`live-a1-loop`, `live-agentctx`, `live-agents`, `live-attachments`, `live-graph`, `live-m9-agents`, `live-restricted`, `live-review-m8-agent`, `live-tool-continuations`, `live-workflow`, `live-workflow-audit`) opt-in; `mathlib-setup` and `mathlib` report SKIPPED unless their environment variables are set, §15; eight review-* scenarios run only when named; 42 scenarios call a debug-only command and need a debug build, §15)
- Workbench: 4 pages, 10 views, 14 editors, 8 status items (7 in the status bar, the project menu in the page bar), 33 dialogs, 9 Settings sections
- Project operations (§8): 3 kinds (delete, duplicate, paste) + restore; 13 target kinds; 9 impact kinds (8 need the one decision); 4 choice keys; 3 recovery kinds; 7 Storage & History categories; 7 Edit commands with focus-scoped bindings
- Project graph: 23 entity kinds, 12 relation kinds, 6 extractors, schema 5; 6 project presets; 2 workflow presets, workflow schemas `exposition.workflow/1` and `/2`, 12 workflow operations with 12 runners (runnable when every runner is available in the open project; the composite runner is always available and its child steps are checked one by one); code in Python, R, or Julia
- Workflow runs: at most 3 active runs (one per workflow), 4 steps in flight (2 restricted, 2 native, 2 agent, 1 Lean); 5 run states, 8 step states, 6 attempt states; 3 run causes; history in `<data>/workflow-runs.sqlite3`; triggers in `<data>/workflow-triggers.json`
- Formats: 4 (Typst, Markdown print profile, pdfLaTeX, LuaLaTeX); 4 page-edit operations
- Research: 3 languages (Python, R, Julia); guided setup of project Python environments (8 `setup.` codes); Lean: 14 document statuses, 45 `lean.` error codes (22 of them `lean.setup.`); guided Mathlib package setup (§5)
- Network: 1 endpoint in app code (`api.anthropic.com/v1/messages`); guided setup's pip downloads from PyPI, and a Mathlib setup's elan, Lake, and Mathlib cache download from the internet (external programs, §12); 18 external tools in the toolchain catalog

## 1. Workbench and interactions

### Pages (4)
The window shows one top-level page at a time. The status bar, notifications, command palette and confirmation dialogs are shared by all pages.

| Id | Title · order | Keys | Contents |
|---|---|---|---|
| `editor` | Editor · 0 (built into the shell) | Mod+1 | The workbench below: sidebar, editor area, bottom panel, auxiliary panel |
| `research` | Research · 20 | Mod+2 | Research page (§4.1) |
| `agents` | Agents · 30 | Mod+3 | Agents page (§6.13) |
| `graph` | Graph · 40 | Mod+4 | Graph page (§7.2) |

- **Switching:** the page bar across the top of the window (UI rework, 2026-11-14): the open project's name (full path as a tooltip; "No folder open" otherwise), then one labeled tab per page (icon + title, `aria-current="page"`, tooltip "<Title> page" + keys; choosing the shown page keeps it shown), then the Manage menu. Also the palette commands "View: Show Editor / Research / Agents / Graph" (`workbench.action.showPage.<id>`, registered per page as pages register). A page shown from a command or key takes keyboard focus (on the Editor page, the editor area); a page-bar click does not move focus.
- **Mounting:** a page mounts on its first visit, then stays mounted, hidden and inert, while another page is shown; each renders inside its own error boundary. The activity rail belongs to the Editor page and is not shown on Research, Agents, or Graph (they use the full width); the region shortcuts (Mod+B/J/Alt+B) used from another page show the Editor page first.

| Region | Contents | What the user can do |

| Bottom panel | Tabs: Problems (10), Research (20), Jobs (30), Toolchains (90) | Switch tab; Hide (Mod+J); "No output" fallback |

| Layout persistence | Region visibility, size, active view, editor split (localStorage) | Auto-collapse auxiliary below 1100 px and sidebar below 860 px window width (not persisted; explicit open overrides) |

### Views (10)
| Id | Region · title | What the user can do |
|---|---|---|
| `workspace.files` | sidebar · Files | No folder: Open Folder…, Recent list (open; × "Remove <name> from Recent"; max 12). Folder open: Refresh, Open Folder…; lazy tree (arrows, Enter/click opens a file tab; link icon; outside-folder/unavailable entries disabled; a folder is listed by one request at a time, requests made meanwhile are answered by one more listing after it, and a listed folder keeps its rows while it is listed again); multi-selection (Ctrl/Cmd+click, Shift+click, Ctrl+A; a plain click still opens a file or toggles a folder) with Copy, Paste, Duplicate, Delete, Delete Permanently by shortcut, context menu "File actions" (right click, Shift+F10, Menu key), or the palette (§8); recovery banner "Unsaved changes from a previous session" with the sentence "Discarded changes cannot be restored. The file on disk stays as it is.": Restore / Discard per file (unreadable: Copy Location / Discard); Discard applies at once. New File… (toolbar, Mod+N; context menu with its folder), Rename… (context menu, F2 with an item focused; dialog listing uses of the old name), OS file drop import with destination highlight (§3.7); no move/cut or internal drag |
| `typesetting.problems` | panel · Problems | Rows (only the open folder's; cleared when a folder closes or another opens) from build-status problems, every diagnostic of each entry's latest build, and Lean diagnostics; severity, source, message, `path:line:col`, hints; click reveals in Monaco and the page view (open files only); "Open Settings → Tool locations" when a hint names it |
| `research.panel` | panel · Research | Environment select (remembered per project) + refresh + setup button (Python: "Install packages…" / "Set up a project environment…"); session pill; Start / Interrupt / Restart / Stop; setup notice, progress, and outcome (§4); code box + Run / Stop / Rerun (Ctrl+Enter runs); output (streams with Show more, figures, inert tables, errors); Cancel run (a queued run or another session's run); Provenance (agent runs: "Authorized"); "Insert or update in document…" (document, caption, insert or update an attachment); run history (the first 200 rows; a last row **Show more runs** · "N records not shown" adds 200 rows and reads the next page of stored runs when the loaded ones are used up; beside the pane title "200 of 1,131 records" while not every record is a listed row: rows shown of runs loaded + records on pages not read + records that cannot be read, so the total does not change when a record on a page read later turns out not to be a run; a row "N run records cannot be read" · "Not listed as runs" when there are such records, and the count then ends at "1,130 of 1,131 records"); Trust workspace… banner |
| `jobs.panel` | panel · Jobs | Newest-first job list; "Show tool probes"; Cancel; details (program, arguments, directory, policy, pid); output stream filter (both/stdout/stderr), Auto-scroll; untrusted refusal → Trust workspace… |
| `jobs.toolchains` | panel · Toolchains | Tables per category (TeX, Python, R, Julia, Lean, Restricted execution, Version control): status, version, location/guidance; Refresh. "Exposition finds tools on this computer and installs nothing automatically." |

Provider settings are in Settings › Model providers |
| `lean.infoview` | auxiliary · Lean | Status pill; Restart file (rebuild imports), Restart Lean server; revision/toolchain/axioms facts; progress; banners (Trust workspace…, Install toolchain, Restart server, Retry now, partially checked, not verified; for a file Lean does not check because its package is not prepared, the reason with **Set Up Mathlib…** and, when only the prebuilt cache is missing (also after an unfinished setup that left the dependency sources as they were), **Build from source instead…**, or the running setup's progress, §5); goals (plain text); "Checked declarations (N)" list (kind, line, name, statement printed with toolchain notation, axioms; Enter/click reveals the name in the editor; generated declarations in a collapsed section; ≤ 300); Incomplete list; axioms / compiled-code list; diagnostics (≤200, click to jump) |
| `graph.dependencies` | auxiliary · Dependencies | Graph items bound at the Monaco caret, their relationships and evidence; Open in Graph / Open in Mathematics (§7.3) |
| `editor.outline` | auxiliary · Outline | Headings and declarations of the active document, pages of its build (§3.7) |
| `editor.equation` | auxiliary · Equation | Visual equation editor (§3.8) |

Agent view sub-screens (task list, composer, task view, approval card, review): see §6.13.

| `media.pdf` | `*.pdf` of the project (read-only PDF tab, §3.7) | source |

Source editor (Monaco): shared document model and shared undo; typing groups until a 1000 ms pause, caret jump, insert/delete switch or line break; banners: changed on disk (Reload from Disk / Keep My Changes), deleted (Save a Copy… / Keep in Editor), save error (Dismiss / Save a Copy… / Retry; Save a Copy… writes the buffer's current text to a new file through `document_save_copy` and never replaces one), mixed line endings (read-only; Normalize to LF / CRLF), read-only reason, NUL characters; unavailable states for binary, undecodable and too-large files; aliases (case variants, short names, links) collapse to one canonical tab. Build diagnostics (Typst, LaTeX, Markdown) appear as squiggles with hover text and hints once a build of the shown revision reports; earlier marks stay and move with edits. Lean adds hover, `\` abbreviation completion, go-to-definition and diagnostics. Links in hovers are copied to the clipboard, never opened ("Link copied" / "Link not opened"); URL detection in source text is not bundled.

| `workspace.project` | page bar | The open project's name; opens the Project menu (§3.7 Project switch) |

### Notifications
Default timeouts: info 6 s, success 5 s, warning 10 s, danger sticky; same id replaces; pause on hover/focus.
- Documents: "Could not undo/redo in X", "An edit to X was not applied", "X changed on disk" / "X was deleted on disk", "X was reloaded", "Recovery backup failed for X", "Could not save X" (sticky), "Could not reload X", "Could not resolve X", "Could not normalize line endings".
- Links: "Link copied", "Link not opened".
- Workspace: "Unsaved changes were recovered" / "Unreadable recovery data" (sticky), "Could not open folder", "Could not reopen <name>", "Could not remove <name> from Recent", "Restored unsaved changes to X" (sticky if the disk changed), "Could not restore unsaved changes", "Could not discard recovered changes", "Location copied", "Could not copy the location".
- Settings: "Invalid application/project/execution settings were reset" (warning, sticky, once per distinct warning), "Could not save editor settings", "Could not save the startup setting", "Restricted execution image set/cleared", "Could not change the container image", "R image set" / "R image cleared" / "Could not change the R image" (and the same for Julia), "Trust revoked", "Could not revoke trust", "<file> now builds with <engine>", "<file> already builds with <engine>", "Could not change the LaTeX engine", "Could not read the project settings".
- Jobs/trust: "Workspace not trusted" (sticky, action Trust workspace…), "Could not cancel job", "Workspace trusted", "Could not trust workspace".
- Page: "Exported PDF" (path); warning "Exported the last successful build" / "Exported an earlier build" (path + "does not include the latest changes") when the exported build is not the current source; "PDF export failed" (reason). A canceled save dialog shows nothing.
- Research: "No script is active", "Nothing selected", "Nothing to rerun", "Research run failed" (only when the window is hidden), "<Figure|Table> inserted into / updated in <path>", "Check the document", "Could not attach the artifact", "Could not cancel the run", "Could not interrupt the kernel", "Could not restart the kernel", "Could not stop the session"; setup: "Environment ready" (success, "<environment> is selected for this project."), "Environment setup failed" (only when the window is hidden), "Could not cancel the setup", "Could not open the page" (Get Python/R/Julia), "<R|Julia> commands copied" / "Could not copy".
- Lean: "Lean is not ready", "No definition found at the cursor", "Defined outside the workspace", "Go to definition failed", "Installing <toolchain>" (action Show Jobs), "Could not install the toolchain", "Could not restart the Lean server", "Could not restart the file".
- Agents: "Agent needs your approval" (sticky, action Open approval; not shown while the card is on screen; dismissed when resolved or the task stops); "<status>: <task>" when a task stops (completed success with "N files to review"; budget exceeded, stopped, interrupted warning; failed, refused danger; canceled shows none); "Project permissions changed" / "Project permissions revoked" when a narrowing stopped Auto tasks or left cleanup unconfirmed ("N Auto tasks stopped; what they did stays reviewable." plus each unconfirmed cleanup; info, or warning and sticky with problems); "Could not open the link" (a response link); "The Research input already has code" (Run in Research did not replace typed code; action Replace input).
- Workflows: "<Title> draft saved" / "Saved revision N" (well formed or incomplete, and whether it can be reviewed and run or why not); when a run ends, once per run and state, "Workflow run succeeded" (success), "Workflow run failed" (danger), "Workflow run canceled" (info), "Workflow interrupted" (warning, "The application stopped during this run; nothing was repeated automatically."), each with action Open run; "Inserted into <path>" after a promotion is approved from a run; "Workflow exported" (success, "<path> (with N workflow(s) it runs). Definitions only: no trust, approvals, triggers, or results."); "Imported “<title>” as a draft" (info, with the number of child workflows saved first; only the root opens); "Run manifest exported" (success, path); "Workflow trigger: suspended: the approved plan no longer matches" / "…: suspended: folder trust was revoked" / "…: suspended: native steps are not allowed" / "…: suspended" (warning, body = the suspension reason, action Open workflow; once per trigger and suspension code, from any page). Enabling, disabling, or firing a trigger shows none.
- Project operations (§8; one per operation): info on full success ("Moved “a.txt” to the Recycle Bin", "Moved 3 items to the Recycle Bin", "Deleted …" for app records, "Permanently deleted …", "Duplicated “x” as “y”", "Pasted 2 items into figures/"; with an action 10 s, else 6 s), warning (sticky) when only some succeeded ("Moved 1 of 2 items to the Recycle Bin"), danger (sticky) when none did ("Could not delete “x”"); the body states the recovery as it is ("It cannot be restored.", "Restore it from the Recycle Bin if needed.", "It can be restored from Settings › Storage & History.") and one line per failed or skipped target with the backend's message, plus what to do for `ops.inUse` ("Close the program that is using it, then try again."), `ops.permissionDenied` ("Check the item's permissions (or whether it is read-only), then try again.") and `ops.interrupted`; actions **Undo** (only when an outcome carries a restore token), **Delete Permanently…** (only for `ops.trashUnavailable` failures of a recoverable delete; a new plan with its own decision), **Copy Reference** / **Copy Text** (`ops.sourceAnchored`), **Delete Source File** (`ops.useSourceFile`). "Nothing to <verb>" (warning, sticky) when every target was refused; "Note" / "Saved version copied" (info, 10 s) for the result's notices; "Nothing was changed" after three plans in a row went stale; Undo reports "Restored “x”", "Restored “x” as “y”", "Restored k of N items", "Could not restore “x”"; an Undo pressed while another operation runs says "Another operation is still running" and keeps the entry. Run outputs held for restore add "Held outputs keep their disk space until you purge them in Settings › Storage & History.". Copying task cards says "Copied the title of “x”" / "Copied N task titles"; copying Research experiments "Copied experiment “x”" ("Pasting on the Graph page makes a separate copy."). Copying files: "Copied “x”" ("Paste into a folder here or in another application.") or, without the system clipboard, "Copied “x” for pasting in Exposition only" (warning); "Nothing to paste" ("The clipboard holds no files." / "The clipboard cannot be read here."). Documents: "Saved a copy of <name>" / "Could not save a copy of <name>".

### Keyboard shortcuts
| Surface | Keys | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Global (registry) | Mod+Shift+P, Mod+1, Mod+2, Mod+3, Mod+4, Mod+B, Mod+J, Mod+Alt+B, F6, Shift+F6, Mod+W, Mod+\, Mod+PageDown, Mod+PageUp, Mod+,, Mod+O, Mod+S, Mod+Alt+S | See §2. Mod = Ctrl (Cmd on macOS). Chords with Ctrl/Alt/F-keys fire in inputs and Monaco unless the editor handled them; text-editing chords (Mod+A/C/V/X/Z/Y, Mod+arrows/Home/End/Backspace/Delete) and commands with scope `workbench` never dispatch while focus edits text (inputs, textareas, contenteditable, Monaco); IME composition and AltGr characters are text |
| Edit commands (registry, scope `workbench`, §8) | Mod+C; Mod+V; Mod+D; Delete (Cmd+Backspace on macOS); Shift+Delete (Cmd+Option+Backspace); Mod+Z; Mod+Shift+Z | Copy, Paste, Duplicate, Delete (recoverable), Delete Permanently, Undo Last Deletion, Redo Last Deletion — only for the focused surface's selection (Files tree, Research explorer, Graph canvases and tables, Workflows list, run lists, agent task lists, workflow canvas); auto-repeated keydowns are ignored for all but Copy; Delete, Delete Permanently, Undo and Redo are disabled while an operation is in flight; Copy with selected text inside the surface stays the browser's; a key whose action is unavailable for the focused item says why (§8.7) |
| Monaco (app actions) | Ctrl+Z; Ctrl+Y, Ctrl+Shift+Z; Ctrl+S; Ctrl+Alt+S | Shared undo; shared redo; Save; Save All |
| Monaco (bundled defaults) | Ctrl+F, Ctrl+H; F8 / Shift+F8; F12, Ctrl+Click (Lean); Ctrl+Space (Lean); Ctrl+/; Alt+↑/↓; Ctrl+D, Alt+Click; Ctrl+Shift+[ / ]; Shift+F10 | Find/replace; next/previous problem; go to definition; suggestions; comment; move line; multi-cursor; fold/unfold; context menu |
| Lean source | `\name` then Space, Tab, `( ) , [ ] { }` or Enter | Expand abbreviation (202 entries; `\\` does not expand); `\` opens a ≤60-entry completion list |
| Editor area (registry, §3.7) | Mod+F; Mod+N; F2 (scope `workbench`, an explorer item focused) | Edit: Find (Source/Split) or Edit: Find in Source (Page); New File…; Rename… |
| Image tab (focused) | Ctrl+wheel; `+` `-` `0`; Shift+1; drag | Zoom at the pointer; zoom in, out, actual size; fit; pan |
| PDF tab (focused) | Ctrl+wheel; `+` `-` `0`; Shift+1; drag; PageUp/PageDown/Home/End/arrows | Zoom; zoom in, out, actual size; fit width; pan; scroll (native) |
| Command palette | type, ↑/↓, Enter, Escape | Filter, move, run highlighted or top match, close |
| Editor tabs | Delete (focused tab), middle-click, double-click | Close, close, pin preview |
| Resize handles | arrows (5 %), Home/End, double-click, F6/Shift+F6 | Resize, min/max, reset, move region focus |
| Dialogs | Escape; Tab (trapped); Enter in path/target fields | Cancel; cycle focus; submit |
| Research code box | Ctrl/Cmd+Enter | Run selection or all code (starts a session if none) |
| Agent request field (Agent view, Agents page) | Enter (also Ctrl/Cmd+Enter); Shift+Enter | Send, as the Send button (never while an IME composes); new line |
| Agent question, Other answer… | Enter | Send answer, as the button (only when every question has an answer) |
| Files tree, Research explorer | arrows, Enter; Ctrl/Cmd+click, Shift+click, Ctrl+A; Shift+F10 / Menu key | Navigate, open file / expand folder; multi-select; context menu under the focused row |
| Context menus (§8) | arrows, Enter, Escape | Move, run, close (focus returns to the item) |
| Approval card | Tab | Payload (only if it scrolls), then Approve, Approve for this task (when offered), Decline |
| Agent response | Tab; Enter | Tab reaches links, the Copy and Run in Research buttons, and code, tables, or display math that overflow (named scrollable regions); Enter on a focused link opens it in the system browser |
| Graph canvas | Space+drag / middle-drag; wheel, pinch, `+`/`-` (`=`/`_`); Shift+1; Shift+2; 0; click, Ctrl/Cmd+click, Shift+drag; Enter/Space; arrows | Pan; zoom; fit to view; fit to selection; reset to 100 %; select, add to selection, box-select; select the focused node or edge; move the selected node (layout only). The Edit commands act on the selected nodes, edges, or table rows (§8) |
| Workflow canvas | `+`/`=`, `-`/`_`; Shift+1; 0; Delete or Backspace; Escape; Enter/Space; arrows; Mod+C, Mod+V, Mod+D | Zoom in, out; fit to view; reset to 100 %; remove the selected connections, steps, inputs, or outcomes from the draft; clear the selection; select the focused node or connection; move the selected node (layout only); copy the selected steps and inputs, paste them into the open draft (new ids, +32 px), duplicate them (§8). Ignored with Ctrl/Alt/Cmd (except the Edit commands) or in text fields; Undo/Redo of deletions is not offered here |
| Page editor | see below | |

### Page-editor interactions (Page and Split modes)
| Interaction | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Click / drag / Shift+click | Hit-test places the caret, extends the selection (drag rAF-throttled); text from another file says "In <path> — open that file to edit it"; generated, code-produced, transformed, raw or package text shows "… — edit in source" |
| Arrows, Home/End, Ctrl+←/→, Ctrl+Home/End | Move by character/line (↑/↓ keep goal x), line start/end, word, document start/end; Shift extends; ←/→ with a selection collapse to its edge; Escape collapses |
| Typing, IME, paste (plain text) | `insert` edit; composition shown live, committed on end; LaTeX special characters inserted escaped |
| Backspace / Delete | Delete backward / forward (Ctrl+Backspace = Backspace; no word delete) |
| Enter (also Shift+Enter) | Paragraph break |
| Copy / Cut | Copies the selection's source text; cut = copy + delete |
| Ctrl+A | Select all |
| Ctrl+Z; Ctrl+Y, Ctrl+Shift+Z | Undo / redo in the shared history (same stack as Monaco and agent edits) |
| Refused edits | Structural edits refused with "would change the document structure — edit in source"; read-only document (e.g. mixed line endings: banner with Normalize to LF / CRLF) refuses edits with its reason |
| Concurrent edits | Keystrokes follow moved positions; if they cannot be placed: "Typing paused: …", nothing applied at a guessed position until the caret is placed again |
| Stale pages | Failed build keeps last good pages with "Showing pages from an earlier revision" + Rebuild; clicking/moving refused, typing still works |
| Toolbar | Status pill (Building / Not trusted / Not built / Build failed (n errors) / Built + ms), Text/Math caret mode, zoom 50–400 % (step 25 %, default 125 %), Export PDF (enabled once pages exist; opens the native save dialog, §3.2) |
| Split view | Source (Monaco) left, page right, 50/50 resizable (min 20 %); one document, edits appear in both immediately |
| Navigation | Problems click places the page caret and the Monaco cursor at the diagnostic; in Page view, a diagnostic the shown pages cannot show (no pages yet, or text added after the last good build) opens Split with the source caret and focus there, the last good pages beside it; no automatic source-cursor ↔ page-caret sync; not handled: PageUp/PageDown, Tab, double/triple-click, context menu, keyboard/Ctrl+wheel zoom, drag-and-drop |

## 2. Command palette

75 registered commands (17 built-in + 4 page commands + 54 feature). The palette lists the 74 other than `workbench.action.showCommands`, only while enabled, as `Category: Title`, ranked by fuzzy match; an empty query shows the session's 20 most recently run first. Keybindings fire only when the command is enabled (an unavailable command lets the key fall through). The 7 Edit commands of §8 set keybinding scope `workbench` and ignore auto-repeat (all but Copy); no other command sets a custom scope. The palette lists the Edit commands for the surface that had focus when it opened.

| Label | Id | Keybinding | When available |
|---|---|---|---|
| View: Show All Commands | `workbench.action.showCommands` | Mod+Shift+P | always (not listed in the palette) |
| View: Show Editor | `workbench.action.showPage.editor` | Mod+1 | always (focuses the editor area) |
| View: Show Research | `workbench.action.showPage.research` | Mod+2 | always (focuses the page) |
| View: Show Agents | `workbench.action.showPage.agents` | Mod+3 | always (focuses the page) |
| View: Show Graph | `workbench.action.showPage.graph` | Mod+4 | always (focuses the page) |
| View: Toggle Sidebar | `workbench.action.toggleSidebar` | Mod+B | always |
| View: Toggle Panel | `workbench.action.togglePanel` | Mod+J | always |
| View: Toggle Auxiliary Panel | `workbench.action.toggleAuxiliary` | Mod+Alt+B | always |
| View: Focus Next Region | `workbench.action.focusNextRegion` | F6 | always |
| View: Focus Previous Region | `workbench.action.focusPreviousRegion` | Shift+F6 | always |
| View: Close Editor | `workbench.action.closeEditor` | Mod+W | an editor is active |
| View: Split Editor Right | `workbench.action.splitEditor` | Mod+\ | an editor is active |
| View: Close Right Editor Group | `workbench.action.closeEditorGroup` | — | right group has editors |

| Research: Rerun the Last Research Run | `research.rerun` | — | the user has run something (shows the Research panel; §4) |
| Research: Interrupt Research Kernel | `research.interrupt` | — | some session is busy |
| Research: Restart Research Kernel | `research.restart` | — | a user session exists |
| Research: Stop Research Session | `research.stop` | — | a user session exists |
| Research: Re-check Python, R, and Julia Environments | `research.refreshEnvironments` | — | always |
| Research: Enable Research for This Project | `research.enable` | — | a folder is open (creates the research space; runs nothing) |
| Agent: New Agent Task | `agents.newTask` | — | always |
| Agent: Show Agent Tasks | `agents.showTasks` | — | always |

| Agent: Open Pending Agent Approval | `agents.openApproval` | — | a task is waiting for approval (opens the first; focuses its card) |
| Agent: Agent Provider Settings | `agents.providerSettings` | — | always |
| Graph: Show Dependencies at Cursor | `graph.showDependencies` | — | always (Editor page, Dependencies view) |
| Graph: Show Project Graph | `graph.showProject` | — | always (Graph page, Project section, focused) |
| Graph: Show Mathematics | `graph.showMathematics` | — | always (Graph page, Mathematics section, focused) |
| Workflow: Show Workflows | `workflows.show` | — | a folder is open (opens the `workflows:` tab "Workflows") |
| Workflow: New Numerical Experiment Draft | `workflows.newNumericalExperiment` | — | a folder is open (saves a new draft from the preset and opens it) |

Engine switch (`latex.*`): changes the engine of the target that builds the active file (default target if it builds that file, else the first LaTeX target for it), or adds a target named after the file; saved to `.exposition/project.json`; open page views rebuild.

## 3. Documents and formats

### 3.1 Formats compared

| | Typst | Markdown (print profile) | LaTeX pdfLaTeX | LaTeX LuaLaTeX |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Entry files | `.typ` | `.md`, `.markdown` | `.tex` (`.ltx`/`.sty`/`.cls`/`.bib` are inputs only) | `.tex` |
| Engine | In-process Typst `0.15.1` | pulldown-cmark 0.13 → Typst via profile `exposition-markdown-print/1` (generated Typst never written to disk) | External `pdflatex` + `bibtex`/`biber` | External `lualatex` + `bibtex`/`biber` |
| Engine choice | — | — | Default for any `.tex`; or a `.exposition/project.json` target for that entry (the `defaultTarget` if it matches, else the first matching target) | Only when a project target for the entry names `luaLatex` (Settings › Project) |
| Dispatch | `typesetting_watch`/`typesetting_build` route by extension: `.tex` → TeX service; `.typ`/`.md`/`.markdown` → typesetting service; any other file → `typesetting.unsupported` (no Typst fallback). `typesetting_export_pdf` routes build ids starting `tex-` to TeX. | same | same | same |
| Build trigger | Page/Split view opens → watch → build now, then on every file change in the folder and on each change of an open document that can concern the entry; Rebuild button; agent `build_document`. A document change leaves an entry alone only when no build of it is queued or unfinished and its latest build is still held and either compiled that revision of the document (or a later one), or had the document open, did not read it, and had no failed read; a document without a path concerns no entry; an unknown document or missed events rebuild every watched entry. Source-only view does not build. A Page tab hidden behind another tab keeps its entry watched (builds, Problems, markers, and the build status stay current) and requests no page image or geometry until it is shown, when it presents the newest result. | same | Watch → Draft build now, on each file change in the folder, and on each change of an open document that can concern the entry; a successful Draft schedules a Full build after 1.5 s idle; `typesetting_build` = Full (waits ≤ 600 s); a newer request cancels the running build and starts after 150 ms without a further request, at most 1 s after the first request of the burst (a request for an idle target starts at once). A document change leaves an entry alone only when no build is pending, running, or canceled since its latest result, the document was an open buffer of that build under the same path and is not a `.bib`, `.bst`, or `biber.conf` file, and either the engine read it and that build compiled this revision (or a later one), or the latest build is a successful Full build, with no Draft since and without Biber, whose recorder shows that the engine did not open the file; everything else rebuilds. Saving Project settings rebuilds watched `.tex` views. Hidden Page tabs as for Typst. | same, except that every change of an open document with a path rebuilds (LuaTeX can read files without a record) |
| Draft / Full | single compile | single compile | Draft: one pass reusing `.aux`/`.bbl`. Full: up to 5 passes; biber if `.bcf`, bibtex if `\bibdata`; reruns on "Rerun…"/label messages or `.aux` change | same |
| Trust | Not required (no child process) | Not required | Trusted folder required; untrusted → build finishes `error` with refusal `untrustedWorkspace` + "Trust workspace…" | same |
| What the build reads | Snapshot: all open buffers (captured atomically) + disk files on demand, inside the canonical root, links checked. Page views read the whole project; agent builds read only the task scope (others read as "access denied"; result not retained for page views) | same | App-owned mirror of the project with open buffers written over it; `TEXINPUTS` falls back to the project; TeX can read any file the account can read | same |
| Packages / fonts | Local packages only (`%APPDATA%\typst\packages`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\typst\packages`); embedded + system fonts; no downloads | Template: New Computer Modern, DejaVu Sans Mono | Installed TeX distribution | same |
| Engine flags | — | — | `-synctex=1 -interaction=nonstopmode -file-line-error -no-shell-escape -recorder`; env `openout_any=p openin_any=p shell_escape=f`; 180 s timeout, 1 MiB log | same |
| Pages shown as | SVG (vector, not rasterized) | SVG | PNG rendered in-process (hayro) at 3 px/pt. A page's reference is its content identity (`tex-c-` + 40 hex characters, over the page object with its content streams, fonts, images, annotations, and inherited settings), so a page that a later build left as it was keeps its reference and is neither rendered nor sent again; a page whose content cannot be identified gets `<build id>-p<index>` | same |
| Diagnostics | Typst errors/warnings with UTF-16 ranges and hints (trace points, package hint) | Translation diagnostics + Typst diagnostics remapped to Markdown ranges; unsupported math → red marker + warning | From the log: errors, undefined references/citations (hint: run a full build), LaTeX/package/class warnings, over/underfull boxes (info), missing characters (one warning per font); whole-line ranges; missing/failed bib tool; "did not run as a TeX engine" | same |
| Squiggles in Monaco | Markers (owner `typesetting`) from the latest build that compiled the editor's current revision; hover = message + hints; kept and moved with edits until a build of the current text reports | same | same (line ranges) | same |
| Missing tool | — | — | Status `toolUnavailable` with guidance; Problems/status bar/page view link to Settings › Tool locations | same |
| Page map | Glyph/character level from Typst spans, verified against source | Character level, Typst map remapped through the Markdown source map | Glyph level for mapped text (SyncTeX line windows + LaTeX source model + PDF glyph alignment, checked glyph by glyph); line level (SyncTeX) otherwise | same |
| Page editing | Prose and equation tokens | Prose, declared LaTeX-math subset, front-matter `title`/`author`/`date` values | Mapped prose and math | same; also combining marks and CJK |
| Failed build | Last good pages kept (`lastGood`), shown stale | same | same | same |
| Retention | 8 recent builds + last good per entry, 64-page SVG cache | same | 8 builds + last good per target; 4 frozen build folders (PDF, SyncTeX, log) per target; 48 cached page mappings per build; rendered page images in one cache of 96 MiB for the service (keyed by page reference and scale, least recently used first) | same |
| Open-folder binding | Build records, pages, page maps, revision texts and exports are served only while the folder that built them is open | same | same; when another folder opens or the folder closes, the workers, builds, and page images of every other folder are released, and build events of a folder that is no longer open are not forwarded | same |
| Edit-to-page time (docs; debug build, Ryzen 7 9800X3D) | ~2–15 ms | ~16–26 ms | ~0.3–0.45 s | ~0.8–1 s |

Markdown front matter: `title`, `author` (scalar or list) → title block + PDF metadata; `date` → title block, and PDF metadata only when `YYYY-MM-DD` is a valid calendar date; `papersize` (a3, a4, a5, b5, letter, legal; default a4); `fontsize` (6–24 pt; default 11); `margin` (cm/mm/in/pt; default 2.5cm); `lang` (default en). Other keys reported and ignored. Images: workspace files relative to the document or `/`-rooted; URLs, `data:`, `mailto:`, `file:`, absolute, backslash, NUL, or escaping paths → warning + marker. Raw HTML prints verbatim with a warning. No citations/bibliography.

### 3.2 PDF export (`typesetting_export_pdf`)
- Bytes: Typst/Markdown = `typst_pdf` from the same layout as the pages; LaTeX = the build's own PDF.
- `typesetting_export_pdf(build, entry, destination?)`. The build must be a retained build of the open folder with pages and a build of `entry` (the document of the page view); otherwise refused before any dialog. An export is an output copy: it saves no buffer, changes no document or project record, and the file is not admitted to the project or the graph.
- UI (Export PDF in the page toolbar): sends the shown build and its document only. The backend opens the native save dialog (title "Export PDF", filter "PDF document", modal to the window, starting in the entry's folder with `<entry stem>.pdf`); the person may choose any location, inside or outside the project. Dismissed → `typesetting.exportCanceled`, nothing written, no notification. Replacing an existing file is confirmed by the system's dialog. If another project is open when the dialog closes: refused, nothing written.
- The exported file is the build shown when Export PDF was pressed. Fresh → "Exported PDF" (path). The latest build failed (last good pages shown) → warning "Exported the last successful build"; the source changed after that build (also while the dialog was open) → warning "Exported an earlier build". Nothing is rebuilt or saved for an export.
- `destination` given (IPC only): accepted only inside the open project; a location outside the project comes from the save dialog only.
- Destination checks (`exposition_workspace::export`): absolute, `.pdf` extension (any case), a valid file name (no device name, `:` stream, trailing dot or space), the folder exists (resolved to its canonical path); never inside the project's `.exposition` or the app data folder. Nothing is created or written elsewhere when a destination is refused.
- An existing destination must be a regular file starting `%PDF-`, not a file the build read (e.g. an included `.pdf` figure), and not an open document (canonical-path comparison). Otherwise refused: "the destination exists and is not a file" / "refusing to replace a file that is not a PDF" / "refusing to replace a source file of the document".
- Write: the shared atomic write (`fsutil::write_atomic`: synced temporary file in the destination folder, then rename; temp removed on failure), so a failed export leaves an existing file as it was. Failures name the file and the reason (permission denied / read-only / open in another program, folder no longer exists, disk full, drive or network location not available) and end "The existing file was not changed." when there is one. Export errors surface as `typesetting.export` (`typesetting.exportCanceled` for a dismissed dialog).

### 3.3 Page edit operations (`page_edit`)
- Ops (only these 4): `insert{text}` (typing, paste, replace-selection), `deleteBackward`, `deleteForward`, `paragraphBreak` (Enter / Shift+Enter). Cut = delete with a selection.
- Outcomes: `applied{revision, caret, mode, rebased}`, `unchanged`, `refused{refusal}`, `stale{currentRevision}` (client maps the selection forward and retries).
- Refusals: `notEditable{reason}` ("not editable on the page (…)"), `wouldChangeStructure` ("…edit in source"), `invalidPosition`, `noChange` (silent).
- `notEditable` reasons — Typst: `code`, `raw`, `comment`, `definition or show rule`, `generated`, `line breaks inside equations`. Markdown: `code`, `raw HTML`, `front matter`, `front matter settings`, `generated content`, `equation delimiters`, `equation structure`, `link or emphasis syntax`, `line breaks inside equations`, `paragraph breaks here`, `table cells`, `footnote definitions`, `setext headings`. LaTeX: `LaTeX markup or generated content`. Any format: `read-only: <reason>`, `the file mixes line endings; normalize them to edit it`, `no page editing for this format yet` (no rules for the extension).
- Transaction: origin `page`, conflict policy rebase-disjoint; consecutive caret typing merges into one undo step (`Typing`), caret deletes merge (`Delete`), Enter = `Paragraph break`; undo/redo shared with the source editor.
- Escaping on insert: Typst escapes markup characters (verified by reparse; three escalation levels); Markdown escapes `\` `` ` `` `$` and context-dependent markers; LaTeX prose: `\` → `\textbackslash{}`, `~` → `\textasciitilde{}`, `^` → `\textasciicircum{}`, `<` `>` `|` → `\textless{}` `\textgreater{}` `\textbar{}`, `"` → ` `` ` (word start) / `''`, `{ } $ & % # _` → backslash-escaped; LaTeX math: `{ } $ & % #` escaped, `\` → `\backslash`, `~` → `\sim`, typed Greek/operators → their macros.
- Refused contexts — Typst: code, raw, comments, `#let`/`#set`/`#show`/closures/loops/imports, generated text. Markdown: code, raw HTML, generated atoms (task boxes, footnote refs, rules), delimiters, `\frac`/`\left` structure, front-matter keys. LaTeX: commands/markup, verbatim (`verbatim`, `lstlisting`, `minted`, `\verb`), comments, `\left`/`\right`/`\big…` delimiters, generated text; Enter only in body prose.

### 3.4 Page images and request limits
- Raster cap: 32 Mi pixels per page image (`MAX_PAGE_IMAGE_PIXELS`); larger LaTeX pages render at a lowered scale. Typst/Markdown SVG is not rasterized.
- `tex_page_image` (debug-only) scale 0.25–8 px/pt; page index < 10 000; build id `tex-…` ≤ 64 chars `[A-Za-z0-9-]`; `page_image` renderRef 1–128 chars `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`; page coordinates must be finite.
- The page view requests images only for the pages in its viewport and the page before and after them (the first 3 pages until it has reported any); every other page keeps a sized box and gets its image when it is displayed. It holds the images of the displayed pages and of the 12 most recently displayed others, of the shown build only, and revokes them all when it detaches.

### 3.5 Format error codes
- `typesetting.`: `unsupported` (not `.typ`/`.md`/`.markdown`/`.tex`), `noWorkspace` (watch with no folder), `build` (could not run; LaTeX not finished in 600 s or channel closed), `export` (every export failure: invalid/refused destination, unknown or mismatched build, I/O), `exportCanceled` (the save dialog was dismissed; nothing written).
- `tex.`: `unsupportedTarget` (not LaTeX with pdfLaTeX/LuaLaTeX), `workspaceNotOpen`, `noTarget` (no LaTeX target and entry not `.tex`), `unknownBuild` (evicted, unknown, or of a folder no longer open), `noOutput` (no pages), `noSuchPage` (index too high or ≥ 10 000), `invalidScale` (outside 0.25–8 or not finite), `invalidDestination` (defined; unreachable over IPC, export reports `typesetting.export`), `io` (I/O or worker join), `invalidBuildId` (not `tex-…`, > 64 chars, bad characters).
- `page.`: `unknownBuild` (build or page map gone), `invalidRef` (bad renderRef), `unknownPage` (page image gone), `invalidPoint` (non-finite x, y or goalX), `internal` (the blocking worker of a page command failed).
- `page_edit` / `document_*` raw errors (`TransactionError`): `empty`, `unknownDocument`, `duplicateDocument`, `readOnly`, `mixedLineEndings`, `staleRevision`, `conflict`, `historyUnavailable`, `futureRevision`, `invalidRange`, `notCharBoundary`, `overlappingPatches`, `nothingToUndo`.

### 3.6 Files
- Encodings: UTF-8, UTF-8 with BOM, UTF-16 LE/BE with BOM; decoded strictly and saved byte-exactly in the same encoding. UTF-32 BOM or dense control characters (≥ 8 and ≥ 1 %) → `file.binary`; invalid bytes (legacy code pages) → `file.undecodable`; not opened as text.
- Size: open and save limited to 10 MiB (`file.tooLarge`); a save that would produce a file Exposition would refuse to reopen (too large or binary-looking) is refused with the buffer kept.
- Line endings: LF, CRLF, none, or mixed, re-detected on every revision. Mixed (incl. any lone CR) is read-only for every origin (source, page, agent, research, system): only a change whose result is consistent, a reload from disk, or a recovery restore is accepted; undo from mixed into other mixed text is refused. `document_normalize_line_endings` (LF or CRLF) is one undoable "Normalize line endings" step. Source editor and page view show "Mixed line endings" with Normalize to LF / Normalize to CRLF.
- Saves: refused if the file changed on disk since load/save (`file.modifiedOnDisk`) unless overwrite; refused for OS read-only files (`file.readOnly`) and folders; written to `.<name>.exposition-save-<unique>.tmp`, synced, renamed over the original; on failure the temp is removed, original untouched, buffer stays dirty. A deleted file is recreated (missing folders created). Save All reports per-file failures.
- Recovery: dirty buffers journaled (≤ every 300 ms) to the app data folder (the journal is written and flushed to a temporary file before the I/O lock is taken and installed under the lock only when the document is still unsaved at that revision with the same file, base, and encoding; otherwise it is written under the lock from the current state, and that document's next 8 journal writes are made under the lock directly); journals removed when the buffer is saved, returns to its saved text, or is closed without saving; kept when the folder closes or the app crashes. Next open of the folder lists them (newest first; disk unchanged/changed/missing/unknown); Restore applies them as one undoable "Restore unsaved changes" step (refused if the open buffer has unsaved changes); Discard deletes the journal at once, without a confirmation (the banner states that discarded changes cannot be restored and that the file on disk stays as it is). Corrupt/newer journals are quarantined (`bad-…`) and shown with their location.
- External changes (watcher, 150 ms settle): clean buffer → reloaded automatically as an undoable `external` step; dirty buffer → conflict banner: modified ("Reload from Disk" = `document_reload`, undoable; "Keep My Changes"), deleted ("Keep in Editor"; buffer marked unsaved, save recreates it), unreadable ("Keep My Changes"); keep = `document_keep_buffer` (next save overwrites); disk matching the buffer again clears the conflict. Failed save → "<name> was not saved" banner with Dismiss / Retry.
- Same physical file reached by another name (link or other spelling) → the same document. Hidden from tree/watcher: `.git`, `.lake`, `node_modules`, `target`, save temps.
- Undo history, per document: 1000 undo steps and 41 MiB for the undo and redo steps together (the text they put back and their records; four full replacements of a 10 MiB document fit). Over the budget whole steps are dropped, never part of one: the oldest undo steps first, then the redo steps furthest from the current text. The step made last always stays, so the last change can be undone (and an undo redone) whatever its size; `canUndo` / `canRedo` report what is left. Whether a document is unsaved is decided by revisions alone (an undo is a new revision), so dropped steps do not change it.
- Rebase history, per document: the patch ranges and text lengths of the newest 2048 revisions, at most 4 MiB (reached only by revisions of thousands of patches: about 17 revisions of 20,000 patches each); the newest revision always stays. An edit based on a revision older than that is refused with `historyUnavailable`.

### 3.7 Editor and explorer (QOL)
- **Find** (`find.ts`): commands `edit.find` "Edit: Find" (Mod+F; Source/Split; Monaco's own find widget on the live buffer) and `edit.findInSource` "Edit: Find in Source" (Mod+F in Page view, page toolbar button: switches to Split, focuses the source, opens Find). Mod+F is taken only when the key event's target is inside the editor area (inside Monaco, Monaco handles it); Files, panels, other text fields and pages keep theirs. No page-text or project-wide search.
- **Outline** (aux view `editor.outline`, "View: Show Outline"; `document_outline_get(document)` → `DocumentOutline` {path, revision, items, coverage}): the graph extractors run on the document's current text (transient; nothing stored, no entity, no event). Items: headings (LaTeX `\part` 1, `\chapter` 2, `\section` 3 … `\subparagraph`; Typst `=` level = count; Markdown ATX/setext) and declarations, nested by heading level, ≤ 5000 headings/rows (more reported), files > 8 MiB "failed". Headings are stripped from the graph's stored per-file index and never feed coverage, matching, or fingerprints. Refresh 300 ms after edits and on save/revert, rename, tab and project switch; answers for another document/path/project/revision are dropped. Navigation re-locates an item by key, first line, and count of identical items; otherwise "<item> is no longer at this place in the source" and the outline refreshes. Pages section: pages of the identified successful build ("Showing pages from an earlier revision" after a failed one; "No pages built yet" + Show Page view).
- **Image tab** (editor `media.image`, priority 20, read-only; `media_read_image(path)` → bytes): project files only (`WorkspaceRoot::resolve_existing`; links out of the folder refused), extension allow-list, ≤ 32 MiB in Rust; format sniffed from content (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, BMP, SVG, AVIF, ICO shown; TIFF, HEIC "unsupported"); rasters ≤ 64 MP and ≤ 32767 px per side from the header before decoding; SVG only as `<img>` from a blob URL; ≤ 3 reads at once, 16 decoded images cached. Fit, Actual size, zoom 10–800 % (Ctrl+wheel at the pointer; `+` `-` `0` Shift+1 when focused), drag/scroll pan. States: missing, outside, too large, unsupported, damaged (UnavailableState), other errors with Retry. Follows replace/delete by watcher events; a renamed image shows "Image not found". Both media commands refuse with `workspace.changed` when the project changes mid-call.
- **PDF tab** (editor `media.pdf`, priority 20, `modes: ["source"]`, read-only; `src/features/media/{PdfViewer.tsx,pdf.ts,useProjectPdf.ts}`, `crates/exposition-tex/src/pdf_view.rs` `PdfViews` in `AppState.pdf_views`): `media_pdf_open(path)` → `PdfViewInfo { token, pageCount, pages: PdfPageSize[], bytes }` reads through `WorkspaceService::read_pdf` (`.pdf` extension only, `resolve_existing`, ≤ 64 MiB `MAX_PDF_BYTES`, `workspace.changed` when the folder changes mid-call) and parses with the LaTeX page view's renderer (`exposition_tex::pdf::PdfDocument`, hayro); `media_pdf_page(token, page, scale)` → PNG bytes (scale 0.25–8 px/pt, lowered to ≤ 16 MP per page; one page rasterized at a time; nothing cached in Rust); `media_pdf_close(token)`. The webview never receives the PDF: pages are `<img>` from blob URLs; no script, action, link, form, or network handling exists in the path. Bounds: first 2,000 pages listed/rendered (info banner beyond), ≤ 4 parsed files and ≤ 128 MiB retained (LRU; a dropped or other-project token answers `pdf.closed` / `workspace.changed` and the viewer opens the file again), ≤ 12 page elements mounted, ≤ 2 page requests in flight, 6 idle page images per view, all revoked on unmount/replace. Parser or rasterizer panics are caught (`pdf.damaged` / `pdf.renderFailed`); a page being rasterized cannot be interrupted. Fit width (default), Actual size (1 pt = 96/72 px), zoom 10–400 %, Previous/Next page, "Page n of N". States: `pdf.notPdf`, `pdf.tooLarge`, `pdf.empty` (0 bytes or 0 pages), `pdf.encrypted`, `pdf.damaged`, missing, outside (UnavailableState); other errors with Retry; a failed page shows its message in the page frame. Follows replace/delete/recreate by watcher events (the shared media change tracker) keeping the old pages until the new result; explorer rename re-keys the tab (`rekeyOtherTabs`); a project switch closes the tab. No text selection or search.
- **Inline previews** (`media_image_references(path, text)` → `ImageReference[]`): the file's extractor on the live text (`UsesAsset` links; comments, code, verbatim, dynamic paths ignored), resolved with the reconciler's candidate rules (`path_candidates`, LaTeX extension search and main-file folders); Monaco view zones (156 px picture, ≤ 480 px wide; 30 px state line), lazy, ≤ 512 px decode; stale answers and loads after a project switch dropped. Palette: "Show Image Previews in Source", "Hide Image Previews in Source", "Show or Hide Image Preview at Cursor"; per-image Hide/Show; stored in `localStorage` `exposition.sourceImagePreviews` (per project file, ≤ 200 files). No edit, dirty flag, undo entry, build, or graph change.
- **File icons** (`src/ui/components/FileIcon.tsx`: `FileIcon`, `fileIconFor`, `fileTypeOf`): one mapping for the Files tree, Research explorer, and editor tabs; 14 px, shape only, decorative beside a name.
- **New File / Rename** (`file_create`, `file_rename_check`, `file_rename`; `exposition_workspace::explorer::validate_name`): codes `name.empty|separator|invalid|trailing|reserved|tooLong|unchanged`, `file.exists` (create-new, MoveFileExW without replace; also a folder of that name), `file.protected` (`.exposition` application data), `path.notFound|notDirectory`, `file.inUse`, `file.changed`, `workspace.changed`. Rename relocates documents at/under the path (`DocumentService::relocate`: same id, revisions, undo, saved state; `language_for` the new name), conflicts, and recovery journals (new written before old removed). The graph records the move in the same serialized step (`GraphService::reconcile_after`, both paths; physical identity match) → `GraphRecording::Recorded{renamed}`; on failure `Pending{message}` (paths stay pending; retried 3× at 0.6/1.2/1.8 s; the watcher's pass reconciles); `Unavailable` without a graph. Debug-only `files_dev_fail_next_graph_record`. On Windows the graph's scan accepts a file only under its on-disk spelling (`scan::stat_file`), so a case-only rename never leaves two resources. Frontend: `editors.rekey` keeps tab position, mode, dirty, active, and close guard; the Monaco view state is restored.
- **OS drop import** (window `dragDropEnabled: true`; `file_import_start` {root, folder, sources, retryOf} → import id; `file_import_cancel`; event `file-import` `FileImportProgress` {import, root, folder, total, done, finished, canceled, items[{source, status, created, code, message}]}): batches of 4 through the P1 orchestrator (paste of `External` targets, plan → apply, journaled; `project-op` per batch); bound to `root` (`import.projectChanged` for batches after a switch); cancel between batches (`import.canceled`); per-import ledger (64 imports) answers `import.alreadyImported` instead of copying a source twice; ≤ 1000 sources. Native drag-drop events (physical position → element), `[data-drop-target]` highlight; progress notification with Cancel above 3 items; result with Retry only for failed, canceled, or interrupted items. With native drop handling on, in-page HTML5 drag-and-drop does not reach the page (no consumer).
- **Project switch** (`switch.ts` state machine; `workspace_open(path, discard?: DocumentId[])`, `CreateProjectRequest.discard?`; `WorkspaceService::switch_workspace` / `check_workspace`): destination first (folder dialog, Recent, New Project form; cancel = no change) → one `confirm` decision only when the open project has unsaved documents or workflow drafts, running workflow runs, or running research code ("Close “A” and open|create “B”"; Cancel · Don't Save · Save / Save All, default Save; only running work: Cancel (default) · Stop and Open / Stop and Create) → Save All (documents, then drafts via the saver registry; any failure stops: "Did not open B — Saved: x. Not saved: y. A is still open.") → commit: the destination is validated and its recovery store prepared before anything of A closes; Don't Save documents are discarded only then; a refused destination: "… A is still open; nothing was closed or discarded." Then what Close Folder does for the previous project (documents close, other unsaved buffers keep journals, workflow runs canceled, research kernels stop, root unregistered), except that the graph is not detached in between: it is attached to the new project directly, so no `detached` status is emitted on a switch. Page-bar project label = Project menu (Open Folder… Mod+O, Open Recent ▸ ≤ 10, New Project…, Close Folder).

### 3.8 Visual equation editor
- **Where**: auxiliary view **Equation** (Σ), with the same operations from the source editor and the exact-page view of LaTeX (`.tex`; pdfLaTeX and LuaLaTeX share the syntax), Typst (`.typ`), and Markdown (`.md`, print profile). Commands: `equation.insertOrEdit` "Insert or Edit Equation" (Mod+Shift+M, only when the key event is in the editor area of a supported document: Monaco or the page input, like Find), "Insert Inline Equation", "Insert Display Equation", "Edit Equation", "Show Equation Panel"; Monaco context menu (Insert Equation, Insert Display Equation, Edit Equation); page toolbar Σ button (Insert Equation, or Edit Equation when the page caret is in math). With no draft the panel offers Insert/Edit and a symbol browser that inserts one symbol directly at the caret (a draft opens instead when the symbol needs a package or the language cannot express it). The surface is the one the entry point names, else the focused one, else the one last focused for that document.
- **Draft**: an editing tree of the equation (`src/features/math/core`: a language adapter parses the content between the delimiters; unknown macros, `\label`/`\tag`/`\nonumber`, comments, spacing, and code become opaque segments kept byte for byte). Structural editor with visible slots and a caret: typing letters/digits, `^`/`_` (scripts on the previous item), `/` (fraction with the previous item as numerator), arrows, Tab/Shift+Tab between slots (Tab leaves at the end), Backspace (a structure with content is selected first), Escape cancels. Templates: fraction, root, n-th root, superscript, subscript, both, integral, sum, product, limit (editable bounds), parentheses, brackets, braces, absolute value, norm, matrix (add/remove row and column), cases, aligned, text. A selection becomes the first slot of a template. Symbol browser: 536 entries in categories (Greek, letterlike and alphabets, operators, relations and negations, arrows, sets, logic, calculus, large operators, delimiters, functions, accents, dots, punctuation, geometry), each with a stable id, name, glyph, aliases, and per-language support; search by name, synonym, glyph, or command (exact, then prefix, word prefix, substring; e.g. "less than or equal", "leq", "≤", "<=" → ≤), keyboard navigation, unavailable entries marked for the language. Preview: KaTeX, labelled approximate; the document's own build is authoritative.
- **Target**: an edit draft targets the whole equation; an insert draft the caret or selection (inside math: no new delimiters, a separating space only when tokens would fuse; in text: the language's inline or display construct). The target range follows every change to the shared document; any change overlapping it (typing, page edits, agent or external edits, undo, a full replacement) or a closed document puts the draft in conflict: nothing is written, the draft stays, and the panel offers "Use current equation", "Move to caret", "Copy draft". Page carets are used only when they map to the current revision with no pending local edits. Switching documents keeps the draft ("belongs to …") until an explicit move or discard.
- **Apply**: one document transaction (`newGroup`, origin `page` when the draft came from the page view, else `source`): the content between the delimiters (surrounding whitespace and indentation kept) plus, when needed and shown (checked by default), the required `\usepackage{…}` line after the preamble's last `\usepackage`/`\documentclass`. Undo/redo from either surface; Cancel, an unchanged draft, or an empty new equation write nothing. Rebuilds and the graph follow the ordinary document path; nothing is stored besides the document text.
- **Support** (Markdown: the print profile's LaTeX subset; anything it cannot render is reported before apply, never emitted):

| Construct | LaTeX | Typst | Markdown |
|---|---|---|---|
| Fractions, roots, scripts, nesting | `\frac` (`\dfrac`/`\tfrac`/`\cfrac` kept), `\sqrt[n]{}`, `^`/`_` | `frac()`, `a/b` (kept), `sqrt()`, `root()`, `^`/`_`, `attach()` | as LaTeX |
| Big operators with bounds | `\int`, `\sum`, `\prod`, `\lim`, … (`\limits` kept) | `integral`, `sum`, `product`, `lim`, … | as LaTeX |
| Delimiters | `\left…\right`, plain pairs | `lr()`, plain pairs, `abs`, `norm`, `floor`, `ceil` | as LaTeX |
| Matrices, cases, aligned | `matrix`/`pmatrix`/`bmatrix`/`Bmatrix`/`vmatrix`/`Vmatrix`, `cases`, `align`-family rows, `aligned` | `mat(delim:)`, `vec()`, `cases()`, `&`/`\` rows | as LaTeX |
| Functions, accents, fonts, text | `\sin`…, `\operatorname`, accents, `\mathbb`/`\mathcal`/`\mathbf`/`\mathrm`/`\mathfrak`/`\mathsf`/`\mathscr`/`\boldsymbol`, `\text` | functions, `op()`, accent functions, `bb`/`cal`/`bold`/`upright`/`frak`/`sans`, `"text"` | as LaTeX where the profile supports it |
| Kept as opaque (not editable) | labels, tags, `\nonumber`, unknown macros, `array`/`alignat`/`smallmatrix`, `\big`/`\middle`, comments | `#code`, comments, unknown names and calls | as LaTeX |

- **Locating equations**: LaTeX `$…$`, `$$…$$`, `\(…\)`, `\[…\]`, `equation`/`align`/`gather`/`multline`/`flalign`/`displaymath`/`math`/`eqnarray` (+`*`), ignoring `\$`, comments, `\verb`, verbatim-like environments, the preamble, and text after `\end{document}`; Typst `$…$` (display when the content starts and ends with whitespace), ignoring comments, raw text, strings, and code; Markdown `$…$`/`$$…$$` under pulldown-cmark rules, ignoring front matter, code spans and blocks, and HTML comments.
- **Limits**: no computer algebra, simplification, or handwriting; no conversion of whole documents between languages; an edited equation is written back in its language's canonical spacing (`\sum_{i=1}^{n}` → `\sum_{i=1}^n`); in LaTeX only same-kind brackets pair (`|x|` stays two symbols); a Unicode operator typed directly in a LaTeX equation becomes its command when that equation is edited; when the page cannot resolve a precise subexpression, the whole equation opens in the panel.

## 4. Research

Python, R and Julia code runs in Jupyter kernels that a bundled Python helper (`exposition_kernel_host.py`, protocol 1) supervises. Sessions need an open, trusted folder. Exposition never installs runtimes or registers kernels; it installs Python packages only into a project environment (`.venv`/`venv`), on the user's explicit Set up environment / Install packages or an agent's setup authority (§6.4), never into a Python installed for the computer.

| Language | Discovered at (Windows; R/Julia take the first match) | Ready when | Kernel |
|---|---|---|---|
| Python | `py.exe -0p`; `python.exe` on PATH; `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Python\pythoncore-*`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Python\Python*`, `%PROGRAMFILES%\Python*`, `%PROGRAMDATA%\Python*`; anaconda3/miniconda3/miniforge3 (also mambaforge in the profile); project `.venv`/`venv` only if trusted | `ipykernel` and `jupyter_client` both import | that interpreter's ipykernel (`exposition-python`) |
| R | `Rscript.exe` on PATH, `%PROGRAMFILES%\R\R-*\bin[\x64]`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\R\R-*\bin[\x64]` | a host Python with `jupyter_client` has an `r` kernelspec (IRkernel) | that kernelspec |
| Julia | `julia.exe` on PATH, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Julia*\bin`, `%USERPROFILE%\.juliaup\bin` | the same, for a `julia` kernelspec (IJulia) | that kernelspec |

- **Discovery:** drops missing files, Store stubs (`WindowsApps`), duplicates and system candidates inside the project. One discovery runs at a time: a request that arrives meanwhile waits and uses the report of a discovery that finished for the same project and trust; a refresh always probes. Probes are 30 s application jobs (`EXPOSITION-PROBE` report); project interpreters run as trusted jobs with `-I`. Unavailable entries show a reason + guidance (Set up environment or Install packages for Python, the official download page for a missing runtime, the exact R/Julia commands) and a structured `setup` hint (`downloadUrl` python.org/downloads, cran.r-project.org, julialang.org/install; `action` `createEnvironment` / `installPackages`; R/Julia `commands` + `commandsFor`, `userWide: true`). Project interpreters carry `projectFolder` (`.venv`/`venv`) and are named "Python <version> (<implementation>, project <folder>)"; a project environment whose setup by Exposition did not finish is listed as "Project environment <folder> (setup not finished)", unavailable, and its interpreter is never run. Cached per (folder, trusted); refresh re-probes.
- **Setup** (`research_setup_plan` → `research_setup_start`, `research_setup_cancel`, `research_setup_status`; event `research:setup`; `crates/exposition-research/src/setup.rs`): `createEnvironment {baseEnvironment, packages}` — a discovered Python outside the project creates `<root>/.venv` (`<base> -I -m venv <root>/.venv`), then `<.venv python> -I -m pip --isolated install --disable-pip-version-check --no-input --require-virtualenv --only-binary=:all: --index-url https://pypi.org/simple ipykernel jupyter_client <packages>`; `installPackages {environment, packages}` — only the pip step, into the project's existing `.venv` or `venv` (a virtual environment with `pyvenv.cfg`; never a system Python; nothing is removed). Packages: a name (≤ 100 characters), optional extras (≤ 10), optional version clauses (`==`, `!=`, `<=`, `>=`, `~=`, `<`, `>`; `.*` only with `==`/`!=`; ≤ 8); URLs, paths, options, markers (`;`), direct references (`@`), `===`, non-ASCII characters, and anything pip would read as a local file (archive or installer suffixes such as `.whl`, `.zip`, `.tar`, `.tar.gz`, `.tgz`, `.egg`, `.exe`, `.msi`, also after extras or a version: "looks like a file name … only package names from PyPI are accepted") are refused; ≤ 200 characters each, ≤ 50 packages including the two kernel packages, one entry per package. The plan (`SetupPlan`: target, base, packages, index, every step's program and argument array, network, summary, `planHash`) runs nothing and never runs a project interpreter: it uses the discovery report already cached for the open project, or else discovers only interpreters outside the project, and recognizes a project environment by its files (`pyvenv.cfg` and its interpreter, not a link, not unfinished); starting re-plans and refuses a different plan (`setup.planStale`). One setup runs at a time in the application (`setup.busy`). Each step is a TrustedNative job of the execution service bound to the project (trusted folder required) that runs in an empty, app-owned working folder `<data>/research/setup-work/<setup id>` (removed afterwards; never the project folder) with `PIP_CONFIG_FILE` set to the null device, so pip reads no configuration file, not even a `pip.ini` inside the environment (the research child environment: the execution service's base variables plus the Python UTF-8/unbuffered settings, no proxy variables; venv 5 min, pip 30 min; log 1 MiB, redacted); switching or closing the project, revoking trust, **Cancel setup**, or the end of the agent task that started it stops it. A created `.venv` gets `.exposition-setup.json` (`exposition.environmentSetup/1`, `inProgress` → `complete`) and, until it completes, an app-local record (`<data>/research/setup-records.json`). A failed or canceled run removes only the folder it created, only when the marker carries the run's token and the folder is not a link; an existing environment is never deleted or replaced, and a failed install into it leaves it as it was ("Nothing was removed from …"). A later setup removes an unfinished `.venv` only when the app-local record matches its marker (otherwise `setup.targetOccupied`). Discovery runs again at the end; the setup succeeds only when discovery reports the environment ready. Codes: `setup.invalidPackage`, `setup.tooManyPackages`, `setup.unsupported` (not a discovered Python, a base inside the project, not a project environment, no `pyvenv.cfg`), `setup.environmentExists`, `setup.targetOccupied` (`.venv` is a link, not an environment, or unfinished without a record), `setup.unfinished`, `setup.planStale`, `setup.busy`; also `research.notTrusted` and `research.workspaceMismatch` (not the open project).
- **Setup UI** (`EnvironmentSetup.tsx`): while the selected environment is unavailable and no session runs, the notice "<environment> is not available" shows discovery's reason and guidance word for word, **Set up environment…** / **Install packages…** (primary; the dialog of §1), **Get Python** / **Get R** / **Get Julia** (the official page in the system browser through `app_open_external`), **Copy R commands** / **Copy Julia commands** ("The R commands install for your user account, not only for this project."), **Check again**; it re-detects when the window regains focus ("Exposition checks again when you come back to this window.") and says "Trust the folder first: setup runs on this computer with your account's privileges." in an untrusted folder. Progress: "Setting up .venv" / "Installing packages into <folder>", "Step n of N: <step>", the output tail, **Cancel setup**. An unsuccessful end: "Setup failed" / "Setup canceled" / "Installation failed" / "Installation canceled" with what was removed or kept, **Set up again…** / **Install packages…**, Show output, Dismiss; success selects the environment ("<folder> is ready").
- **Remembered environment** (`research_selection_get` / `research_selection_set`): the environment the user chooses (or a successful setup selects) is remembered per canonical project root in `<data>/research/selections.json` (≤ 200 projects, the least recently chosen forgotten first; only a discovered id is accepted). After discovery the selection is the session's current choice if still discovered, else the remembered one, else the first usable environment, else the first listed.
- **Run loop** (`RunControls`, panel and Research page): **Run** (Play), **Stop** (Square: cancels the user's running code), **Rerun** (RotateCcw: the last user run's kind of input against the current source — the same file as it is now, open buffer first; the current selection of the same document; or the Research input as it is now, else the last input's code; accessible name e.g. "Rerun analysis/loop.py"); Interrupt (OctagonPause) and Restart (RefreshCcwDot) use their own icons. Run, Rerun, and Run in Research from an agent response (§6.13) are the user's direct actions: no extra dialog, trust still required, nothing granted to agents. Palette: Stop the Running Research Code, Rerun the Last Research Run.
- **Start / launch:** open folder + trust + a discovered environment; ≤ 8 user and 4 agent sessions (within the execution service's 16 interactive jobs, shared with Lean). `<host python> -u -X utf8 <helper>` is a TrustedNative interactive job in the folder: scrubbed env (`JUPYTER_RUNTIME_DIR=<data>/research/runtime`), 64 KiB redacted log, helper at `<data>/research/kernel-host/0.1.0-<hash12>/` (hash re-checked each launch). R/Julia get the folder as byte data (`rawToChar(as.raw(..))` / `hex2bytes`), never spliced into code.
- **States:** Starting, Idle, Busy, Restarting, Crashed{reason} (helper silent 20 s then killed, helper exit, kernel died; active run → Failed, queued → Canceled), Detached{reason} (final: folder switched/closed or trust revoked; kernel stopped, runs canceled), Stopped (final).
- **Owners:** UI = User. The agent `run_python` tool creates `Agent{task}` sessions (first ready Python; reused while live; all stopped when the task ends). `Workflow{run, step}` owns workflow batch runs (§7.7), which use no kernel session and are listed with the project's other runs. Only `execute` checks requester = owner. On app exit helpers die with the execution service's job shutdown.
- **Run kinds:** Input `{code}` (non-empty, ≤ 4 MiB); Selection `{document, revision, UTF-16 range}` (revision must be current, else `staleSource`); File `{path}` (open buffer, else hashed saved bytes; under the session's folder; extension `py`/`ipy`/`pyw`, `r`, `jl` must match). One active run per session, FIFO queue; ids `run-<16 hex>`.
- **Run statuses:** Running, Succeeded, Failed{message} (error, kernel died, helper stopped; a manifest left Running reads as "Exposition stopped before this run finished."), Canceled (aborted, restarted, shut down, cancel, trust revoked), Interrupted.
- **Outputs:** Stream (stdout/stderr), Display, Error (ename, evalue, traceback), ClearOutput, Notice; `text/plain` inline (≤ 16 Ki chars, ANSI stripped). **Artifacts:** Figure (first of PNG, JPEG, GIF, SVG per display), Table (first HTML `<table>` → CSV ≤ 500 rows × 50 cols, notice when cut), Data (HTML kept as inert `.html`). Limits: 200 files and 64 MiB/file per run, streams 8 Mi chars/run, live retention 2 MiB / 10,000 entries, output pages 200 (max 500), history ≤ 500 manifests. A finished run counts as stored once its manifest and transcript are on disk and the transcript read back holds exactly its entries; the output and code of the 16 most recent stored runs of the open project stay in memory, older stored runs and, after a project switch or close, every stored run of another project are released and read from their records (the history lists them unchanged; output that arrives late is written to the record first, and output, a display artifact, or an error that arrives after the release is added to the record: transcript and manifest), and a run whose record could not be written keeps everything in memory. Output read from a record is checked against the manifest's entry count: a missing or damaged transcript of a run that had output is an error, and one that ends early is marked `truncated`. The webview keeps the output of the selected run, of runs still executing, and of the 8 newest other finished runs; selecting any other run reads its whole output again (≤ 40 pages of 500 entries).
- **Storage:** `.exposition/runs/<run>.json` (`exposition.run/1`: inputs + revision + sha256, session/interpreter/kernel/helper provenance, code sha256 + text ≤ 256 KiB, error, output summary, and since M8 an optional `execution` record of the mode the run actually ran in: `trustedNative{interpreter}` or `restrictedContainer{backend, image, imageId, network, user, memory, cpus, pidsLimit, timeLimitMs}`; absent = trusted native), and since A1 an optional `authorization` for an agent's native `run_python` run that needed one (`{principal: "agent", taskId, mode: "ask"|"auto", basis: "approved"|"taskAllowance"|"projectPermissions", permissionsVersion?}`; vocabulary and task id validated, unknown fields refused; set only by the agents service for its own task's run, never by the webview; shown as "Authorized" in Provenance and on the Research inspector, e.g. "Agent task <id> · Auto: project permissions v3"); `.exposition/artifacts/<run>/output-N.<png|jpg|gif|svg|html|csv>` (workflow batches: `<name>.<ext>`, §7.7) + `transcript.json`. Files must be direct children of the run folder; size and sha256 recomputed; bytes served only on request, never in events.
- **Redaction:** streams, errors, notices, logs, and text artifacts (HTML/SVG/CSV/JSON, rewritten on disk with `[REDACTED]`) before registration; images untouched.
- **Run history** (`research_runs`, `RunHistoryPage`): read in pages. The first page holds every run of this app session and the 500 most recently modified stored records; each further page (`after` = the previous page's `next`) the next 500 (`limit` 1–500). Pages follow the records' modification times, newest first, then id; within a page, and in the list, runs are ordered by start time, newest first. `hasMore` and `next` say whether stored runs remain; `total` counts this session's runs and every record file; `unreadable` counts the record files found so far that are in no page: files that are not a manifest of their run (remembered by modification time, size, file identity and change time, read again only when one of them changes; a repair that keeps time and size is listed by the next page, whether it replaces the file or rewrites it in place, since every write and every change of the file's times moves its change time; two rewrites within one tick of the file system's clock that also keep size and time are not told apart), and files that could not be opened (held by another program: tried three times, never remembered as damaged, read again by the next page that reaches them).
