| Generated outputs (`generatedOutputs`) | `runOutputs` of research runs (also deleted records) that have outputs ("Outputs of … (N file(s), k kept by the project)") | only the files a deletion would remove | some output is kept by the project / running | "Files runs produced. Deleting removes the files nothing keeps (restorable until purged); the run record stays. Outputs retained or attached to a manuscript are kept and not counted; delete those files themselves." |
| Agent task records (`agentTasks`) | tasks of the open project (other projects' tasks are neither listed nor purged here) | stored size | changes awaiting review / running | "… after a permanent deletion those changes can no longer be reverted from the task." |
| Workflow revisions (`workflowRevisions`) | revisions other than the current one ("…, run by …" when pinned) | stored size | pinned / — | "Revisions other workflows run are marked and kept unless you include them." |
| Recently deleted (`recoverableDeletions`) | recoverable deletions (runs, tasks, graph items, workflows, held run outputs "N output file(s) of run …"), newest first | record or file size | — | "Items deleted in Exposition that can still be restored. Deleting them here removes them for good." |
| Disposable records (`caches`) | one item: the unreferenced tombstone records | not measured | — | "Metadata kept for files that no longer exist; removing it changes nothing you can see." |

The page shows each category with count and size ("not measured" when unknown), rows with Include checkboxes, size, created date, Referenced/Active badges, and **Restore** on recently deleted rows; **Select unreferenced** never adds referenced items; the scope bar shows "N selected · X of files (+k not measured)" and "Purge removes the selected records and payloads permanently. Removing records frees database space only when the database is compacted; the result says what was actually reclaimed.", and names included referenced or active items; **Purge N Selected** sends the whole selection as ONE permanent-delete request (records and their outputs are separate targets; duplicates collapse), so a batch gets one combined decision when it needs one. "Not included here" lists what is not purged: graph backups (when present: "… may still contain deleted or purged items, workflows and relationships; the newest 8 are kept and older ones are replaced over time."), "Files deleted to the Recycle Bin stay there until you empty it; items purged here are not copied anywhere else.", exported bundles and manifests and copies in version control or sync folders, "Deleting removes records and files from disk; it is not secure erasure.", and the run history database's size. No retention policy runs automatically.

### 8.5 Workflows
- **Duplicate / Paste (Workflows list):** a new workflow id, revision 1, title "<title> (copy)", fresh step ids (`<prefix>_<6 hex>`) with connections, conditions, and outcomes remapped; inputs and composite pins kept as written (external references stay explicit); saved as an ordinary, non-imported draft whose runnability is recomputed. No triggers, runs, approvals, verdicts, or trust are copied, and nothing runs. A single revision duplicates the same way. A deleted workflow must be restored before it is copied.
- **Delete:** removes the workflow from active use and turns every trigger of it off (`triggers` choice when one is enabled; with `skip` the workflow is kept), stops an active run only when chosen; run history stays; restorable (§8.4). When the deletion does not happen after its triggers were turned off (the graph deletion fails), each trigger is put back exactly as it was: an active one listens again, a suspended one stays suspended with its reason, the "Turned off" history entry is taken back, and nothing is recorded as enabled; the same holds for the triggers a file deletion turned off when that deletion is refused, skipped, or fails (§8.3). The Workflows list closes clean editor tabs of deleted workflows. A composite whose pinned child is deleted keeps resolving the pinned revision until it is purged; after a purge the composite reads Incomplete (`workflow.compositeMissing`, checked whenever it is read) and cannot run.
- **Workflow canvas** (open draft): Copy (selected steps and inputs with their configuration and the connections among them; outcomes are not copied; app clipboard `exposition.workflow-fragment/1`), Paste into Draft (new ids `<id>-copy`, `<id>-copy2`, …; labels "(copy)" when taken; +32 px offset; connections, prerequisites, annotations, and conditions inside the selection remapped; links to items outside it kept only within the same workflow, others dropped and listed: "Not carried over: …"), Duplicate, Remove from Draft (Delete/Backspace, also several items). Changes stay in the unsaved draft until Save; nothing runs.

### 8.6 Project graph items
Labels are specific. **Hide from view** is presentation only (the saved view's hidden list; nothing is deleted). **Delete Relationship**, **Delete Entity**, and **Delete Source File/Folder** differ:
- A file-backed item (a resource or folder that still exists): the UI deletes the source file itself through a `path` target ("Source file moves to the Recycle Bin"); a `graphEntity` target for it is refused `ops.useSourceFile` ("… is the file …: delete the source file itself (it would reappear when the project is indexed again).").
- Refused entities: the project and research space (`ops.protected`), workflow entities (`ops.useWorkflow`: delete or duplicate them from the Workflows list). Tombstoned items ("Removed from source") and curated items (also with region bindings) are deletable: the item, its bindings, and every relationship touching it are removed and do not come back on reindex; evidence records are never removed (their verdicts stay as history). The decision appears only when the item provides recorded evidence for a surviving item.
- **Delete from Source** (items extracted from or declared in a source — LaTeX theorem-like and proof environments, Lean declarations, BibTeX entries, Typst/Markdown declarations — while active; the orchestrator's source side, never the data side): ordinary Delete removes the item's TEXT from its source through the document service, as ONE unsaved transaction per file (origin `source`, a new group in the shared undo history; label "Delete “…” from the source"). Nothing is written to disk; the graph follows when the file is saved (the graph reads an unsaved buffer only as an overlay). The plan locates the item in the file's CURRENT text — an open document's buffer, else the file decoded as `document_open` would (`WorkspaceService::read_text`, nothing opened) — by re-extracting it: a named declaration by its syntax and name; an unnamed one (a proof) by its recorded fingerprint, else the only construct with exactly its text, else its recorded range when the text is exactly the one the graph read. Anything else is not guessed: `ops.sourceChanged` ("<path> has unsaved changes around “…”, so it cannot be located exactly. Save <path> (the graph reads it again) and try again, or remove it in the editor." / "… is declared more than once in <path>; …"), `ops.notFound` ("… is no longer in <path> …"), `ops.sourceDerived` (a binding that is not resolved, a file no extractor reads); the notification then offers **Open Source**. The removal is the whole construct (a Lean declaration with its doc comment and attributes); when it stands on lines of its own, those whole lines plus one separating blank line when it had blank lines on both sides (a last block takes the gap before it); several removals in one file merge. Effect "Remove “…” from <path>, lines a–b as an unsaved edit (review it and save; Undo in the editor brings it back)", recovery `sourceEdit` (outcome `edit {path, document, revision, at}`; code `ops.sourceEdited`, "Removed from <path>, lines a–b[ with its proof] as an unsaved edit: review it and save (Undo in the editor brings it back). The graph follows when you save."). Its proofs are removed with it unless the person keeps them (`proofs` choice); other users of it and literal Lean name mentions are `sourceDependents` impacts, so the decision appears only then. Lean mentions: whole dotted identifiers outside comments and strings in every project `.lean` file (open buffers as they are; `.lake`, `.git`, `.exposition`, `node_modules`, `target`, `build`, `.elan` skipped; ≤ 2000 files, ≤ 50 mentions per file) that name the declaration by a suffix of its full name (`basel`, `LeanBasic.basel`, `basel.symm`), attributed to the declaration containing them — `open` and local names are not resolved, so it is a list to review, not a dependency analysis. The plan's fingerprint covers each file's text: any edit before applying makes the plan stale (nothing removed); apply re-checks the text per file. Refused: Delete Permanently (`ops.notSupported` "… is removed from its source as an edit you review and save; use Delete (it can be undone in the editor)."), agents (`ops.agentScope`: they change sources with their file tools, as reviewed edits), an item whose source file the same request deletes (`ops.covered` "Its source <path> is deleted in this operation."), several declaration bindings. Undo: Ctrl+Z in the editor, or **Undo Removal** in the notification (one step of each document's history, only while the document is still at the revision the removal produced; otherwise "Undo it in the editor"); not on the operations' Undo list.
- Relationships: observed ones and structural kinds (`contains`, `anchoredIn`, `imports`) are refused (`ops.sourceDerived`, "… to change it, change the source."); a curated relationship, an `evidenceFor` link included, is deleted at once (restorable) and stays deleted across reindexing; relationships are not duplicated alone (`ops.notSupported`).
- **Duplicate** only for curated items with an independent-copy meaning: "<label> (copy)", no key or binding, attributes copied plus `copy.fromEntity`; accepted hand-made relations among the copied items are remapped, relations to items outside the selection kept only for `dependsOn`, `assumes`, `references`, `derivedFrom`; nothing uses the copy; no evidence or verification is copied (`ops.invalid` if the copy fails validation). Refused: source-anchored items (`ops.sourceAnchored`, "… is anchored in its source; duplicating it would need a source edit. Use Copy Reference or Copy Text instead."), references and external resources (`ops.sourceAnchored`, a second card would duplicate one source's identity), file-backed items (duplicate the file). The UI offers **Copy Reference** (key or label) and **Copy Text** (the exact source text, from an open document) instead.
- Saved views: delete (restorable; layout only) and duplicate ("… (copy)").
- The old "Remove this relationship?" confirmation is gone; "Archive <item>?" remains for archiving.

### 8.7 Commands, focus, and feedback (UI)
`src/features/operations/` (client, decision, clipboard, surfaces, menus), `src/ui/components/ContextMenu.tsx`, and each surface's module (`workspace/fileOps.ts`, `research/researchOps.ts`, `graph/graphOps.ts`, `workflows/workflowOps.ts` + `subgraph.ts`, `agents/taskOps.ts`, `settings/StorageSection.tsx`).

| Command (palette "Edit: …") | Id | Windows / Linux | macOS |
|---|---|---|---|
| Copy Selected Items | `operations.copy` | Ctrl+C | Cmd+C |
| Paste Items | `operations.paste` | Ctrl+V | Cmd+V |
| Duplicate Selected Items | `operations.duplicate` | Ctrl+D | Cmd+D |
| Delete Selected Items (recoverable) | `operations.delete` | Delete | Cmd+Backspace |
| Delete Selected Items Permanently | `operations.deletePermanently` | Shift+Delete | Cmd+Option+Backspace |
| Undo Last Deletion | `operations.undo` | Ctrl+Z | Cmd+Z |
| Redo Last Deletion (deletes again, recoverably, what Undo restored) | `operations.redo` | Ctrl+Shift+Z | Cmd+Shift+Z |

- **Focus:** the commands have scope `workbench`, so they never fire while focus edits text (inputs, textareas, contenteditable, Monaco — where Ctrl+D, Ctrl+Z, Delete stay the editor's — or a terminal's input); they act only when the focused element is inside a registered surface and stands for its selection (a tree row, a canvas node or edge, a table row, a card; not a toolbar button, a tab, or the search field); focus on the page body or in a dialog, menu, or popover → nothing acts. The palette lists them for the surface that had focus when it opened. Auto-repeated keydowns are ignored for all but Copy; Delete, Delete Permanently, Undo, and Redo are disabled while an operation is in flight; IME composition is ignored; selected text inside a surface keeps the browser's Copy. Undo/Redo are not offered on the workflow canvas.
- **Surfaces:** Files tree, Research explorer (Paste into Sources/Data/Notes or beside a file; experiments copy to the graph's app clipboard; references are not duplicated), Graph Project and Mathematics canvases and tables, Workflows list, run lists, Agents page and panel task lists (cards: click/Space select, Ctrl/Cmd+click adds, **More actions** button), workflow canvas.
- **Context menus** (right click, Shift+F10, Menu key; right click on an unselected item selects it alone): Copy · Paste · Duplicate │ surface extras (Hide from view, Copy Reference, Copy Text, Delete Run and Its Outputs, Delete Run and Its Outputs Permanently…) │ Delete · Delete Permanently (danger). Each shows its binding while enabled (not in the graph Inspector's menu, where the keys do not act) and its recovery ("Moves to the Recycle Bin", "Disables its triggers; restorable from Storage & History", "The record only; outputs stay", "Cannot be restored"); unavailable items stay visible, disabled, with the reason. Specific labels: Paste into `<folder>/` (selected folder, or the selected file's parent; "the project folder" with nothing selected), Delete Source File/Folder, Delete from Source (description "Removes its text from the source as an unsaved edit you review and save"; its Delete Permanently is disabled: "… use Delete from Source"), Delete Entity, Delete Relationship, Delete Run Record, Delete Task Record, Remove from Draft, Paste into Draft. Runs: Copy is unavailable ("A run is not copied; review a new run from its workflow"); **Delete Run and Its Outputs** (outputs held for restore; "never inputs or files the project keeps") and **Delete Run and Its Outputs Permanently…** send the record and its `runOutputs` as two targets in one request (one decision). Tasks: Copy copies the title as text; Duplicate is unavailable ("A task is not duplicated; start a new task to reuse its instructions"); no Paste.
- **Keys on items that cannot take them** (Delete, Delete Permanently, Duplicate, or Paste with a focused item whose action is unavailable — the project node, a workflow node, an observed relationship, a read-only graph, Shift+Delete on a source item): an info notification "<action> is not available" with the menu's reason, at most once per reason every 2 s (`CommandContribution.disabledReason`, `unavailableReasonForEvent` in `src/app/keybindings.ts`); never in text fields.
- **Removed from a source:** the result opens the Editor at the first removal point (`revealSource`, now `src/features/editor/reveal.ts`), title "Removed “…” from its source", actions **Show in Editor** and **Undo Removal**; the decision is titled "Delete “…” from its source", its impacts under "Used elsewhere in the sources".
- **Decision and feedback:** the decision dialog and notifications of §1; `ops.planStale` on apply re-plans and shows the decision again with "The items or their consequences changed since this was planned.", keeping the choices made before (at most 3 tries). Only one operation runs at a time: Undo (also from a notification) and Redo wait their turn, and a Redo cancelled at its decision stays available. Undo appears only when an outcome carries a restore token; Undo of an occupied location asks (**Restore with a New Name** / **Leave Deleted**). The Undo list is kept per project in localStorage `exposition.operations.undo:<project root>` (≤ 20 entries: titles, targets, names, and tokens, never content), so Undo works after a restart while the token is valid; the Redo list is not kept.

### 8.8 Codes and platform coverage
- `ops.` (40): `sourceEdited` (a removal from a source) and `sourceChanged` (not located exactly), and request and plan `noProject`, `invalidRequest`, `planUnknown`, `planStale`, `failed`, `interrupted`; refusals `outsideProject`, `notFound`, `notSupported`, `protected`, `readOnly`, `intoItself`, `linkNotCopied`, `agentScope`, `nameUnavailable`, `alreadyDeleted`, `latestRevision`, `useSourceFile`, `useWorkflow`, `sourceDerived`, `sourceAnchored`, `invalid`; outcomes `inUse`, `permissionDenied`, `trashUnavailable`, `partial`, `cancelled`, `covered`, `activeWork`, `triggerEnabled`; restore `restored`, `restoreUnavailable`, `restoreCollision`, `restoreFailed`, `restoreIncomplete`, `restoreUnknown`, `restoreConflict`; clipboard `clipboardUnavailable`. Pass-through: `workflow.notDeleted`, `agents.taskActive`, `agents.alreadyDeleted`, `agents.notDeleted`, `research.runActive`, `research.restoreConflict`, `research.unknownOutputs`, and other service codes; `document.*` for Save a Copy.
- **Windows (verified):** Recycle Bin delete and restore with reliable matching (files and folders, an occupied original path, a junction trashed without touching its target), a locked file failing with `ops.inUse` and never deleted permanently, access denied → `ops.permissionDenied`, read-only refusal, `mklink /J` junctions never followed or copied, eight concurrent pastes, the `CF_HDROP` file list read and written (also with Explorer-style `Get-Clipboard`/`Set-Clipboard` in e2e), restore tokens surviving a restart. A drive without a Recycle Bin (`ops.trashUnavailable`) is covered only with a simulated bin.
- **macOS:** Delete moves to the Trash but issues no restore token (no Undo; restore from the Trash); restore commands fail; bin errors are `ops.failed` (never `ops.trashUnavailable`); the file clipboard is unavailable (`ops.clipboardUnavailable`). **Linux (freedesktop trash):** matching and restore tokens are implemented but not verified; the file clipboard is unavailable. On Unix a file without write permission counts as read-only. Neither platform is verified.

## 9. Settings

`<data>` = `EXPOSITION_DATA_DIR`, else `%LOCALAPPDATA%\org.exposition.desktop`. Settings editor sections: Appearance, Editor, Project, Trusted folders, Execution, Agents, Research, Storage & History (P1, §8.4: an overview with purge and restore; no stored setting and no automatic retention policy), About. Project operations add no setting: their bindings are fixed (§8.7). Workflow runs add no setting: their limits come from the operation registry and the plan (§7.7), the concurrency limits are fixed in the application, restricted batches use the Execution `docker.*` settings, and the run history is `<data>/workflow-runs.sqlite3` (§13). A trigger's quiet period and rate limits are per trigger, chosen when it is enabled and stored with it in `<data>/workflow-triggers.json` (§7.8), not settings.

### App (`<data>/settings.json`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | u32 | 1 | — (must be 1) |
| `files.reopenLastProject` | bool | false | Settings › Project › Startup "Reopen the last project" |

### Editor (same file, `editor.*`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `editor.fontSize` | u32 (8–40) | 13 | Settings › Editor › Font size (10–24 px list) |
| `editor.tabSize` | u32 (1–16) | 2 | Settings › Editor › Tab size (2, 3, 4, 8) |
| `editor.insertSpaces` | bool | true | Settings › Editor › Indent with spaces |
| `editor.wordWrap` | bool | true | Settings › Editor › Wrap long lines |
| `editor.lineNumbers` | bool | true | Settings › Editor › Line numbers |
| `editor.minimap` | bool | false | Settings › Editor › Minimap |
| `editor.defaultEol` | `lf` \| `crlf` | `lf` | Settings › Editor › Line ending for new lines (files without line endings only) |

- Missing file → defaults; missing fields → defaults; invalid file → copied to `settings.json.invalid-<ms>` (original removed only if the copy verifies) and defaults used; startup notification "Invalid application settings were reset". Writes atomic (fsync + rename). Recent folders live separately in `<data>/recent-projects.json` (max 12; Remove from Recent).
- Reopen last project: once per application start (sessionStorage flag survives webview reloads); skipped if a folder is already open; failure → "Could not reopen <name>".

### Project (`<project>/.exposition/project.json`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | u32 | 1 | — (must be 1) |
| `entry` | workspace path \| null | null | — (used by the debug-only `tex_build` with no entry when no default target) |
| `targets[]` | `{name, format, engine, entry}` | [] | Settings › Project › LaTeX targets (Add target…, Edit…, Remove, per-row Engine select); palette `latex.*` |
| `defaultTarget` | target name \| null | null | Settings › Project › Default target (None or any target) |

- Rules: names trimmed, 1–64 chars, unique; pairs typst/typst, latex/pdfLatex, latex/luaLatex, markdown/markdownPrint; default must name a target. UI adds: main file relative, inside the folder, valid Windows name, ends `.tex`. A `.tex` file without a target builds with pdfLaTeX. Typst/Markdown targets are kept but not editable in the UI.
- Invalid file → `project.json.invalid-<ms>` beside it, defaults, banner in Settings › Project + notification (also via the workspace event when the file changes). No folder open → "No folder open" state.

### Execution (`<data>/execution/settings.json`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `toolPaths` | map tool id → absolute path | {} | Settings › Execution › Tool locations (Set…/Change…, Reset, Re-check tools) |
| `docker.image` | string \| null | null | Settings › Execution › Container image (local images only; Clear, Reload images); used by restricted agent runs and Python workflow batches |
| `docker.rImage` | string (absent when unset) | absent | Settings › Execution › R image (local image providing `Rscript`; Clear); restricted R workflow batches only |
| `docker.juliaImage` | string (absent when unset) | absent | Settings › Execution › Julia image (local image providing `julia`; Clear); restricted Julia workflow batches only |
| `docker.memory` | string | `2g` | — (file only; ≥6m) |
| `docker.cpus` | string | `2` | — (file only; 0.01–1024) |
| `docker.pidsLimit` | u32 | 256 | — (file only; 1–4194304) |
| `docker.user` | `uid:gid` | `65534:65534` | — (file only; numeric, non-root) |
| `maxConcurrentJobs` | u32 \| null | null (= CPUs/2 clamped 2–8) | — (file only; 1–64) |

- Webview can change only `dockerImage`, `languageImage {language: r | julia, image?}` (M9; empty clears; validated like the image, presence not required; unset → "No R image: restricted R batches are refused; they never run natively instead."), and `toolPath`. Image: Docker reference grammar, ≤512 chars; presence not required to save (missing shows "Not present locally"). Tool path: catalog tool (15 overridable: pdflatex, lualatex, latexmk, biber, bibtex, synctex, python, rscript, r, julia, lean, lake, elan, docker, git; kernel components follow their interpreter), absolute, no `..`, existing file, `.exe` on Windows (`.bat`/`.cmd`/`cmd.exe`/`.ps1` refused), passes the launch gate, not inside an open project or `<data>/execution`; re-checked at every use.
- Load: unparsable file or invalid limits/user/job limit → each bad value replaced by its default, original kept as `settings.json.invalid-<ms>`, corrected file written; if the backup fails the original stays until a backup succeeds. Banner in Settings › Execution + startup notification "Invalid execution settings were reset". Writes atomic (fsync + rename).

### Provider (`<data>/agents.sqlite3`, table `settings`, key `provider`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `provider` | `anthropic` \| `openai` \| `scripted` | `anthropic` | Settings › Model providers › Defaults for new tasks › Model and effort (the model menu lists every configured provider's models; scripted only in debug builds; stored scripted reads as anthropic in release) |
| `model` | string (1–100 of `A-Za-z0-9 . - _ : @ /`) | `claude-opus-5-5` | Settings › Model providers › Defaults for new tasks › Model and effort |
| `effort` | `low` \| `medium` \| `high` \| `xhigh` \| `max` | `medium` | Settings › Model providers › Defaults for new tasks › Model and effort |
| `consent` | `metadataOnly` \| `scopeContent` | `scopeContent` | Settings › Model providers › Defaults for new tasks › Content sent to the model provider |
| `openai` | `{organization?, project?}` | absent | Settings › Model providers › OpenAI › Organization ID, Project ID (§6.1) |
| API key (secret, one per provider) | 16–512 printable ASCII | none | Settings › Model providers › Save key / Remove; Windows Credential Manager `anthropic-api-key.org.exposition.desktop` / `openai-api-key.org.exposition.desktop` (one entry per provider), local-machine persistence; debug builds use `….development` unless `EXPOSITION_CREDENTIAL_NAMESPACE` or `EXPOSITION_USE_REAL_CREDENTIAL=1`; never returned to the webview |

- Corrupt stored JSON → defaults. Settings › Model providers also holds the Project permissions list (below; §6.13).

### Trust (`<data>/execution/trust.json`)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | u32 | 1 | — |
| `roots` | list of canonical folder paths | [] | Settings › Trusted folders (list, Refresh, Revoke…); granted only via the "Trust this workspace?" dialog |
| `granted_at_ms` | map path → ms | {} | Shown as "Trusted <date>" / "Trusted (date not recorded)" |

- Entries show "Folder no longer exists" and "Ignored: too broad" pills; "What can be trusted" lists personal and system folders. Unreadable file → nothing trusted. Writes atomic.

### Project permissions (`<data>/execution/project-permissions.json`, A1)
| Key | Type | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `version` | u32 | 1 | — (must be 1) |
| `lastVersion` | u64 | — | — (largest record version ever issued, revocations included) |
| `projects[]` | `{root, project, version, grantedAtMs, updatedAtMs, files, code, setup}` | [] | Composer › Approvals › Auto (Use these permissions, Change…, Revoke); Settings › Model providers › Project permissions (Change… for the open project, Revoke for any) |

- `root` = canonical project folder, `project` = project-graph identity; a record matches only both (§6.5). `files` `off`/`edit`, `code` `off`/`restricted`/`native`, `setup` bool (only with `code: native`). `version` ≥ the current time in ms and larger than every earlier one, also after the file was lost. Never in the project, never imported, never inferred from graph labels; changed only through `agents_permissions_set` / `agents_permissions_revoke`. Unknown fields, a relative root, setup without native code, or a record version above `lastVersion` make the file invalid: nothing is granted, the original is copied to `project-permissions.json.invalid-<ms>` before anything overwrites it (if that copy fails, the file is left unchanged and changes are refused). Writes atomic. Native code running with the user's privileges can still edit this file.

### Research environment choice (`<data>/research/selections.json`, A1)
`{"format":"exposition.researchSelections/1","projects":{<canonical root, lowercased on Windows>:{environment, updatedAtMs}}}`; ≤ 200 projects (the least recently chosen dropped); written via a temporary file + rename; unreadable → renamed to `selections.invalid-<ms>` and nothing remembered. UI: the Research environment select (§4).

### Setup records (`<data>/research/setup-records.json`, A1)
`{"format":"exposition.setupRecords/1","unfinished":[{target, token, createdAtMs}]}`: one entry per `.venv` a setup created and has not finished; removed when the setup completes or its environment is removed. A project cannot plant one, so an unfinished `.venv` is removed by a later setup only when its marker's token matches (§4). No UI.

### Lean setup records (`<data>/lean/setup-records.json`)
`{"format":"exposition.leanSetupRecords/1","records":[{root, created[], interrupted, sourcesIntact}]}`: one entry per package root while a Mathlib setup runs there (what it will create, recorded before it exists) and afterwards only when the setup may have left the package incomplete; removed when a setup of that package succeeds or cleans up completely. A project cannot plant one. A recorded package that uses Mathlib is not ready and Lean does not start on it, except by the user's build-from-source choice when `sourcesIntact` (absent in older records = false) and the dependencies are still complete; a package that no longer uses Mathlib is not held by its record (§5). No UI.

### Frontend-only state
| Key | Storage | Default | UI location |
|---|---|---|---|
| `exposition.theme` | localStorage | `"system"` | Settings › Appearance › Color theme; Manage menu; theme palette commands |
| `exposition.workbench.layout` | localStorage | sidebar 260 px, panel 240 px, auxiliary 320 px (all visible), split 50 % | Drag/keyboard resize, toggles, View: Reset Layout |
| `exposition.startupReopenAttempted` | sessionStorage | absent | — (one reopen attempt per application start) |
| `exposition.graph.mode.project` | localStorage | `"canvas"` | Graph page › Project › Display (Canvas / Table) |
| `exposition.workflows.view` | localStorage | `"form"` | Workflow editor › Form / Canvas |
| `exposition.workflows.layout.<workflow id>` | localStorage | absent | Workflow canvas: moved node positions (removed by Arrange); presentation only |
| `exposition.operations.undo:<project root>` | localStorage | absent | Undo list of project operations per project (≤ 20 entries: titles, targets, names, restore tokens; never content), §8.7 |

In-memory only: the app clipboard of project operations (files, workflows, graph items, workflow-canvas fragments) and the Redo list, Jobs "Show tool probes", selected Settings section (default Appearance), Files tree expansion, job output buffers, Graph page section (Project / Mathematics) and Mathematics view (Dependencies / Verification). Graph layouts (positions, viewport, filters, hidden, collapsed) are saved views in the project graph, not browser storage (§7.2).

## 10. IPC endpoints (webview → core)

Manifest `src-tauri/build.rs` (`COMMANDS` 193 release, `DEV_COMMANDS` 16 debug-only); handlers `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` `generate_handler!` (209 = 193 + 16 `#[cfg(debug_assertions)]`); grants `src-tauri/capabilities/default.json` (capability `main-window`, window `main`: exactly the 193 `allow-*` plus `core:event:default`, `core:window:allow-set-title`, `core:window:allow-destroy`, `dialog:allow-open`; no `dialog:allow-save`: the export and Save a Copy save dialogs are opened by the backend). Args are camelCase over IPC. Errors: `CommandError{code,message}`; document commands and `page_edit` reject with `TransactionError{kind}`; Lean commands with `LeanError{code,message,currentRevision}`; graph errors arrive as `CommandError` with the graph's `graph.*` code; workflow run errors (`WorkflowRunError{code,message}`) as `CommandError`; project operations with `OpError{code,message}` (`ops.*`). The number in each heading counts the release commands of that group (they add up to 193). The commands no interface calls are debug-only and listed last; they do not exist in a release build.
### App and documents (8)
| Command | Args | Returns | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| app_info | — | AppInfo | Name, version, OS, arch, debug flag, data dir, Typst compiler, Markdown profile |
| app_open_external | url: String | () | Open a link the user chose (agent-response links, Get Python/R/Julia) in the system's default browser (Windows `ShellExecuteW`; `open` / `xdg-open` elsewhere). Absolute `http`/`https` with a host, no user name or password, no whitespace, control, invisible, or direction-changing characters, ≤ 2048 characters; else `app.invalidUrl`; a browser that cannot start → `app.openFailed`. The only external-navigation route: the `navigation-guard` plugin (`src-tauri/src/app.rs`) refuses any navigation of the main window except the app's own content (`tauri:`, `http(s)://tauri.localhost`, `about:blank`; debug builds also the Vite dev server `localhost:1420`) |
| document_list | — | DocumentInfo[] | Documents open in the document service |
| document_state | document: DocumentId | DocumentState | Full text and info of one open document |
| document_apply | request: TransactionRequest | TransactionOutcome | Apply revisioned edit; refused e.g. `mixedLineEndings`, `readOnly`, `conflict`, `staleRevision` |
| document_undo | document: DocumentId, origin: EditOrigin | DocumentChanged | Undo one step of the shared history (`nothingToUndo`) |
| document_redo | document: DocumentId, origin: EditOrigin | DocumentChanged | Redo one step of the shared history |
| document_outline_get | document: DocumentId | DocumentOutline{path, revision, items, coverage} | Outline of the document's current text (headings and declarations; nothing stored); `document.unknown`, `outline.untitled` (§3.7) |

| Command | Args | Returns | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|

| lean_associate_declaration | request: LeanAssociateRequest{declaration: audited{document, name} \| entity{entity}, target, note? ≤500} | LeanAssociation | Reviewed `formalizes` relation (accepted; an existing suggestion is accepted); an audited name records its evidence first (`lean.notAudited`); `lean.notDeclaration`, `lean.associationRefused` |

| lean_setup_plan | request: LeanSetupRequest (`newMathlibPackage{folder, name}` \| `prepareMathlibPackage{folder}`) | LeanSetupPlan | Describe a Mathlib setup (files, toolchain, revision, lakefile and manifest digests, sources, every command); runs nothing; `lean.setup.*` refusals |
| lean_setup_start | plan: LeanSetupPlan | LeanSetupStatus | Start a reviewed plan: re-plans and refuses a changed one (`lean.setup.planStale`); trusted folder (`lean.untrusted`); one at a time (`lean.setup.busy`) |
| lean_setup_cancel | setupId: String | bool | Cancel the running setup (false when it is not running) |
| lean_setup_status | — | LeanSetupStatus \| null | The running Mathlib setup, else the last finished one of this session |
| lean_allow_local_build | document: DocumentId | () | The user's choice to check the document's package without Mathlib's prebuilt cache (Lake builds Mathlib from source), until the app closes |
### Agents (30)
| Command | Args | Returns | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| agents_provider_status | — | ProviderStatus | Settings, key configured, credential store name/error, scripted available |
| agents_provider_update | settings: ProviderSettings | ProviderStatus | Save provider, model, effort, default consent; emits `providerChanged` |
| agents_provider_set_key | key: String (≤1024), provider: ProviderKind? (default `anthropic`) | ProviderStatus | Store that provider's API key in Credential Manager; never returned |
| agents_provider_clear_key | provider: ProviderKind? (default `anthropic`) | ProviderStatus | Delete that provider's stored key |
| agents_models_list | provider: ProviderKind, refresh: bool? | ModelCatalog | The models a connection offers, fetched in Rust with its key (§6.1) |
| agents_attachments_pick | — | IngestOutcome[] | Attach files: the backend opens the native file dialog and snapshots the chosen files (§6.1.1) |
| agents_attachments_add_bytes | name: String?, mediaType: String?, data: String (base64) | IngestOutcome | Attach pasted bytes (a clipboard image, a large text paste) |
| agents_attachments_add_dropped | paths: String[] (≤ 100) | IngestOutcome[] | Attach files of a native drop on the chat: only paths the desktop shell recorded for a drop in the last 60 s, each once (`attachments.notDropped`, `attachments.folder`, `attachments.tooMany`) |
| agents_attachment_preview | id: AttachmentId | String \| null | Thumbnail data URL of an image attachment (`attachments.invalid`) |
| agents_attachments_discard | ids: AttachmentId[] | () | Forget draft attachments removed before sending |
| agents_task_list | — | TaskSummary[] | All tasks |
| agents_task_detail | task: TaskId, sinceSeq?: number | TaskDetail | Summary, transcript (tool calls with `authorizedBy`), pending approvals (with `reason`, `forTask`), graph record (context, targets, proposals, investigation), `allowances`. With `sinceSeq` a partial read: `transcriptFrom` is set, `transcript` holds only the items from that sequence number on, and `graph.context` is left out; everything else is complete. The webview uses it for the events of a task whose detail it holds (from the latest assistant item, or an earlier unfinished tool call) and reads the whole detail when it holds none or `graph.contexts` differs |
| agents_task_create | request: CreateTaskRequest | TaskSummary | Validate and start a task (prompt ≤100,000 chars, open folder only; `approvalMode` `auto` needs the project's permissions: `agents.permissionsRequired`) |
| agents_task_continue | task: TaskId, prompt: String, budgets: Budgets?, attachments: AttachmentId[]?, provider: ProviderChoice? | TaskSummary | Follow-up on a stopped task, optional new budgets, attachments, and an explicit model switch (§6.1); `agents.taskRunning` while the task runs, `agents.taskFinishing` while a finished turn is still closing (§6.2) |
| agents_task_cancel | task: TaskId | TaskSummary | Cancel the stream or running tool |
| agents_approval_resolve | task: TaskId, approval: String (≤128), approved: bool, scope: ApprovalScope? (`once` default \| `task`) | () | Approve or deny a pending tool call (`agents.noApproval`); `task` = "Approve for this task" (`agents.notRepeatable` when the request is once-only; it stays pending) |
| agents_question_answer | task: TaskId, request: String (≤128), revision: u32, answers: QuestionAnswer[] (≤3) | () | Answer a structured question (§6.7.1): `agents.invalidAnswer`, `agents.questionStale`, `agents.questionResolved`, `agents.noQuestion`; an identical second answer is Ok |
| agents_question_discuss | task: TaskId, request: String (≤128), revision: u32, text: String (≤4 000 chars) | () | "Chat about it": one bounded discussion turn in the task's worker (`agents.questionBusy` while one runs) |
| agents_figure | task: TaskId, runId: String, artifactId: String (≤128 each) | bytes (ArrayBuffer) | A figure recorded on one of the task's own tool calls, read through the research artifact store with its size and SHA-256 checked; otherwise `agents.figureUnavailable` (not this task's, purged, missing, changed, or the task's project is not open) |
| agents_review | task: TaskId | TaskReview | Checkpoint and changed files with review state; each file's `unsaved` (open document with unsaved changes; absent when not open or the task's project is not open) |
| agents_review_diff | task: TaskId, path: WorkspacePath | FileDiff | Original, modified, checkpoint texts of one file |
| agents_review_revert | task: TaskId, path: WorkspacePath? | ReviewActionResult[] | Revert one or all files; `agents.taskRunning` while active |
| agents_review_accept | task: TaskId, path: WorkspacePath? | ReviewActionResult[] | Accept one or all pending files; `agents.taskRunning` while active |
| agents_task_keep_investigation | task: TaskId | EntityId | Keep a stopped task as an investigation in the project graph (idempotent) |
| agents_task_create_generation | request: GenerationTaskRequest | TaskSummary | Start a new project's generation task (whole project, execution disabled by default) |
| agents_permissions_get | root: String (≤4096, no NUL; the open project) | ProjectPermissionsState | The open project's permissions record (or null), its graph identity, the defaults to offer, `restrictedAvailable`, `trusted` (§6.5) |
| agents_permissions_list | — | ProjectPermissionsEntry[] | Every record with `exists` and `open` (Settings › Model providers) |
| agents_permissions_set | root: String, choice: PermissionChoice{files, code, setup} | PermissionsChange{root, project, permissions, narrowed, stopped, problems} | Grant or change the open project's permissions (`agents.invalidPermissions`, `agents.graphUnavailable` without an attached graph); a narrowing stops the project's active Auto tasks (waits ≤ 10 s); emits `permissionsChanged` |
| agents_permissions_revoke | root: String, project: String? (≤128) | PermissionsChange | Revoke a project's record (it need not be open; `agents.notFound` without one); stops its active Auto tasks; emits `permissionsChanged` |
| agents_allowance_revoke | task: TaskId, kind: AllowanceKind? | () | Revoke one allowance of a task, or all (`null`) |
